import { Module } from '@nestjs/common';
import { ConfigModule, ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';
import {
  TypeOrmModule,
  TypeOrmModuleOptions,
} from '@nestjs/typeorm';

import databaseConfig from './config/database.config';
import jwtConfig from './config/jwt.config';
import { ProductsModule } from './modules/products/products.module';
import { OrganizationModule } from './modules/organization/organization.module';
import { BranchesModule } from './modules/branches/branches.module';
import { AuthModule } from './modules/auth/auth.module';

@Module({
  imports: [
    ConfigModule.forRoot({
      isGlobal: true,
      envFilePath:
        process.env.NODE_ENV === 'test'
          ? '.env.test'
          : '.env',
      load: [
        databaseConfig,
        jwtConfig,
      ],
    }),

    TypeOrmModule.forRootAsync({
      imports: [ConfigModule],
      inject: [ConfigService],
      useFactory: (
        configService: ConfigService,
      ): TypeOrmModuleOptions =>
        configService.getOrThrow<TypeOrmModuleOptions>(
          'database',
        ),
    }),

    OrganizationModule,
    BranchesModule,
    AuthModule,
    ProductsModule,
  ],
})
export class AppModule {}

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\main.ts
==================================================

import { ValidationPipe } from '@nestjs/common';
import { NestFactory } from '@nestjs/core';

import { AppModule } from './app.module';
import { setupSwagger } from './config/swagger.config';

async function bootstrap(): Promise<void> {
  const app = await NestFactory.create(AppModule);

  app.setGlobalPrefix('api');

  app.enableCors();

  app.useGlobalPipes(
    new ValidationPipe({
      whitelist: true,
      forbidNonWhitelisted: true,
      transform: true,
    }),
  );

  setupSwagger(app);

  const port = process.env.PORT ?? 3000;

  await app.listen(port);

  console.log(`Application running on: http://localhost:${port}/api`);
  console.log(`Swagger documentation: http://localhost:${port}/api/docs`);
}

void bootstrap();

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\common\decorators\noLeadingSpecialChars.ts
==================================================

import {
  registerDecorator,
  type ValidationOptions,
  type ValidationArguments,
} from 'class-validator';

export function NoLeadingSpecialChars(
  validationOptions?: ValidationOptions,
): PropertyDecorator {
  return (target: object, propertyKey: string | symbol) => {
    registerDecorator({
      name: 'NoLeadingSpecialChars',
      target: target.constructor,
      propertyName: propertyKey.toString(),
      options: validationOptions,
      validator: {
        validate(value: unknown): boolean {
          if (typeof value !== 'string') {
            return false;
          }

          return /^[\p{L}\p{N}]/u.test(value.trim());
        },

        defaultMessage(args: ValidationArguments): string {
          return `${args.property} must start with a letter or number.`;
        },
      },
    });
  };
}

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\common\helpers\error.helper.ts

import {
  BadRequestException,
  ConflictException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { QueryFailedError } from 'typeorm';

interface PostgresDriverError {
  code?: string;
}

export function throwDatabaseError(error: unknown): never {
  if (!(error instanceof QueryFailedError)) {
    throw error;
  }

  const driverError =
    error.driverError as PostgresDriverError | undefined;

  if (!driverError?.code) {
    throw new ConflictException(
      'A database operation could not be completed.',
    );
  }

  switch (driverError.code) {
    case '23505':
      throw new ConflictException('Resource already exists.');

    case '23503':
      throw new ConflictException('Resource is still in use.');

    case '22P02':
      throw new BadRequestException('Invalid identifier.');

    case '22003':
      throw new BadRequestException('Numeric value is out of range.');

    default:
      throw error;
  }
}

import type { TransformFnParams } from 'class-transformer';

export function trimString({ value }: TransformFnParams): unknown {
  return typeof value === 'string' ? value.trim() : value;
}

export function lowercaseString({ value }: TransformFnParams): unknown {
  return typeof value === 'string' ? value.trim().toLowerCase() : value;
}

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\common\request-metadata\getUser.decorator.ts

import {
  createParamDecorator,
  ExecutionContext,
} from '@nestjs/common';

import type { RequestUser } from '../interfaces/request-user.interface';

interface AuthenticatedRequest {
  user: RequestUser;
}

export const GetUser = createParamDecorator(
  (
    _data: unknown,
    context: ExecutionContext,
  ): RequestUser => {
    const request =
      context.switchToHttp().getRequest<AuthenticatedRequest>();

    return request.user;
  },
);

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\common\services\crypto.service.spec.ts
==================================================
import { CryptoService } from './crypto.service';

describe('CryptoService', () => {
  let service: CryptoService;

  beforeEach(() => {
    service = new CryptoService();
  });

  describe('hashPassword', () => {
    it('should return a hashed password instead of the plain password', async () => {
      const password = 'StrongPassword123!';

      const result = await service.hashPassword(password);

      expect(result).toBeDefined();
      expect(result).not.toBe(password);
      expect(result).toMatch(/^\$2[aby]\$/);
    });

    it('should generate different hashes for the same password', async () => {
      const password = 'StrongPassword123!';

      const firstHash = await service.hashPassword(password);
      const secondHash = await service.hashPassword(password);

      expect(firstHash).not.toBe(secondHash);
    });
  });

  describe('comparePassword', () => {
    it('should return true when the password matches the hash', async () => {
      const password = 'StrongPassword123!';
      const passwordHash = await service.hashPassword(password);

      const result = await service.comparePassword(
        password,
        passwordHash,
      );

      expect(result).toBe(true);
    });

    it('should return false when the password does not match the hash', async () => {
      const passwordHash = await service.hashPassword(
        'StrongPassword123!',
      );

      const result = await service.comparePassword(
        'WrongPassword123!',
        passwordHash,
      );

      expect(result).toBe(false);
    });
  });
});

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\common\services\crypto.service.ts
==================================================

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import * as bcrypt from 'bcrypt';

@Injectable()
export class CryptoService {
  private readonly saltRounds = 10;

  async hashPassword(password: string): Promise<string> {
    return bcrypt.hash(password, this.saltRounds);
  }

  async comparePassword(
    password: string,
    passwordHash: string,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    return bcrypt.compare(password, passwordHash);
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\common\services\transaction.service.spec.ts
==================================================
import { DataSource, EntityManager } from 'typeorm';

import { TransactionService } from './transaction.service';

describe('TransactionService', () => {
  let service: TransactionService;
  let dataSource: jest.Mocked<Pick<DataSource, 'transaction'>>;
  let manager: EntityManager;

  beforeEach(() => {
    manager = {} as EntityManager;

    dataSource = {
      transaction: jest.fn(),
    };

    service = new TransactionService(
      dataSource as unknown as DataSource,
    );
  });

  describe('runInTransaction', () => {
    it('should execute the callback inside a transaction', async () => {
      const expectedResult = { id: '123' };

      dataSource.transaction.mockImplementation(
        async (callback: any) => callback(manager),
      );

      const callback = jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(expectedResult);

      const result = await service.runInTransaction(callback);

      expect(dataSource.transaction).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
      expect(callback).toHaveBeenCalledWith(manager);
      expect(result).toEqual(expectedResult);
    });

    it('should propagate errors thrown by the transaction callback', async () => {
      const error = new Error('Transaction failed.');

      dataSource.transaction.mockImplementation(
        async (callback: any) => callback(manager),
      );

      const callback = jest.fn().mockRejectedValue(error);

      await expect(
        service.runInTransaction(callback),
      ).rejects.toThrow(error);
    });
  });
});

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\common\services\transaction.service.ts
==================================================

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  DataSource,
  EntityManager,
} from 'typeorm';

@Injectable()
export class TransactionService {
  constructor(
    private readonly dataSource: DataSource,
  ) {}

  async runInTransaction<T>(
    callback: (manager: EntityManager) => Promise<T>,
  ): Promise<T> {
    return this.dataSource.transaction(callback);
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\config\database.config.ts
==================================================

import { registerAs } from '@nestjs/config';
import type { TypeOrmModuleOptions } from '@nestjs/typeorm';

function getRequiredEnv(name: string): string {
  const value = process.env[name]?.trim();

  if (!value) {
    throw new Error(
      `[Configuration Error] Missing required environment variable: ${name}`,
    );
  }

  return value;
}

function getDatabasePort(): number {
  const port = Number(getRequiredEnv('DB_PORT'));

  if (!Number.isInteger(port) || port < 1 || port > 65535) {
    throw new Error(
      '[Configuration Error] DB_PORT must be a valid port number.',
    );
  }

  return port;
}

export default registerAs(
  'database',
  (): TypeOrmModuleOptions => {
    const nodeEnv = process.env.NODE_ENV ?? 'development';
    const databaseName = getRequiredEnv('DB_NAME');
    const isTest = nodeEnv === 'test';

    // Never allow dropSchema to run against a non-test database.
    if (isTest && !databaseName.endsWith('_test')) {
      throw new Error(
        '[Database Safety Error] Refusing to enable dropSchema. ' +
        'The test database name must end with "_test".',
      );
    }

    return {
      type: 'postgres',
      host: getRequiredEnv('DB_HOST'),
      port: getDatabasePort(),
      username: getRequiredEnv('DB_USER'),
      password: getRequiredEnv('DB_PASSWORD'),
      database: databaseName,

      autoLoadEntities: true,

      // Development and isolated test databases only.
      synchronize: nodeEnv === 'development' || isTest,

      // Destructive schema reset is permitted only for the test DB.
      dropSchema: isTest,
    };
  },
);

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\config\jwt.config.ts
==================================================

import { registerAs } from '@nestjs/config';

export default registerAs('jwt', () => {
  const secret = process.env.JWT_SECRET?.trim();

  if (!secret) {
    throw new Error(
      '[Configuration Error] Missing required environment variable: JWT_SECRET',
    );
  }

  const expiresIn =
    process.env.JWT_EXPIRES_IN?.trim() || '1h';

  if (!/^\d+(ms|s|m|h|d|w|y)$/.test(expiresIn)) {
    throw new Error(
      '[Configuration Error] JWT_EXPIRES_IN must be a valid duration, such as 15m, 1h, or 7d.',
    );
  }

  return {
    secret,
    expiresIn,
  };
});

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\config\swagger.config.ts

import { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  DocumentBuilder,
  SwaggerModule,
} from '@nestjs/swagger';

export function setupSwagger(app: INestApplication): void {
  const config = new DocumentBuilder()
    .setTitle('Branchwork API')
    .setDescription(
      'Multi-tenant, multi-branch organization management API',
    )
    .setVersion('1.0')
    .addBearerAuth()
    .build();

  const document = SwaggerModule.createDocument(app, config);

  SwaggerModule.setup('api/docs', app, document, {
    jsonDocumentUrl: 'api/docs-json',
  });
}

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\modules\auth\auth.controller.ts
==================================================

import {
  Body,
  Controller,
  Get,
  Post,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  ApiBearerAuth,
  ApiOperation,
  ApiTags,
} from '@nestjs/swagger';

import { GetUser } from '../../common/request-metadata/getUser.decorator';
import type { RequestUser } from '../../common/interfaces/request-user.interface';

import { Public } from './decorators/auth.decorator';
import { AuthService } from './auth.service';

import { RegisterDto } from './dto/register.dto';
import { LoginDto } from './dto/login.dto';

@ApiTags('Authentication')
@Controller('auth')
export class AuthController {
  constructor(
    private readonly authService: AuthService,
  ) {}

  @Post('register')
  @Public()
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: 'Register an organization and its owner',
  })
  async register(@Body() dto: RegisterDto) {
    return this.authService.register(dto);
  }

  @Post('login')
  @Public()
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: 'Authenticate a user',
  })
  async login(@Body() dto: LoginDto) {
    return this.authService.login(dto);
  }

  @Get('me')
  @ApiBearerAuth()
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: 'Get the authenticated user profile',
  })
  async getProfile(
    @GetUser() requester: RequestUser,
  ) {
    return this.authService.getProfile(requester.id);
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\modules\auth\auth.module.ts
==================================================

import { Module } from '@nestjs/common';
import { APP_GUARD } from '@nestjs/core';
import { ConfigModule, ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';
import { JwtModule } from '@nestjs/jwt';
import { PassportModule } from '@nestjs/passport';
import type { SignOptions } from 'jsonwebtoken';
import { CommonModule } from '../../common/common.module';

import { OrganizationModule } from '../organization/organization.module';
import { UsersModule } from '../users/users.module';
import { RbacModule } from './rbac.module';

import { AuthController } from './auth.controller';
import { AuthService } from './auth.service';
import { JwtStrategy } from './strategies/jwt.strategy';
import { JwtAuthGuard } from './guards/jwt-auth.guard';
import { AccessGuard } from './guards/access.guard';

@Module({
  imports: [
    ConfigModule,
    CommonModule,
    PassportModule,
    JwtModule.registerAsync({
      imports: [ConfigModule],
      inject: [ConfigService],
      useFactory: (configService: ConfigService) => ({
        secret: configService.get<string>('jwt.secret'),
        signOptions: {
          expiresIn: configService.getOrThrow<string>(
  'jwt.expiresIn',
) as SignOptions['expiresIn'],
        },
      }),
    }),
    OrganizationModule,
    UsersModule,
    RbacModule,
  ],
  controllers: [AuthController],
  providers: [
    AuthService,
    JwtStrategy,
    JwtAuthGuard,
    AccessGuard,
    {
      provide: APP_GUARD,
      useExisting: JwtAuthGuard,
    },
    {
      provide: APP_GUARD,
      useExisting: AccessGuard,
    },
  ],
  exports: [AuthService],
})
export class AuthModule {}

import {
  ForbiddenException,
  UnauthorizedException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { JwtService } from '@nestjs/jwt';

import { CryptoService } from '../../common/services/crypto.service';
import { TransactionService } from '../../common/services/transaction.service';
import { PERMISSION_CATALOG } from '../../common/constants/permissions.constants';
import { OrganizationService } from '../organization/organization.service';
import { UsersService } from '../users/users.service';
import { RbacService } from './rbac.service';

import { AuthService } from './auth.service';

describe('AuthService', () => {
  let service: AuthService;

  let jwtService: jest.Mocked<
    Pick<JwtService, 'signAsync'>
  >;

  let cryptoService: jest.Mocked<
    Pick<CryptoService, 'hashPassword' | 'comparePassword'>
  >;

  let transactionService: jest.Mocked<
    Pick<TransactionService, 'runInTransaction'>
  >;

  let organizationService: jest.Mocked<
    Pick<OrganizationService, 'create'>
  >;

  let usersService: jest.Mocked<
    Pick<
      UsersService,
      'createOwner' | 'findByEmail' | 'findAuthUserById'
    >
  >;

  let rbacService: jest.Mocked<
    Pick<RbacService, 'findRoleById'>
  >;

  const organizationId = 'organization-123';
  const userId = 'user-123';
  const roleId = 'role-123';
  const manager = {} as any;

  const owner = {
    id: userId,
    organizationId,
    name: 'John Owner',
    email: 'owner@example.com',
    userType: 'OWNER',
    passwordHash: 'hashed-password',
  };

  const staff = {
    id: 'staff-123',
    organizationId,
    name: 'John Staff',
    email: 'staff@example.com',
    userType: 'STAFF',
    branchId: 'branch-123',
    roleId,
    passwordHash: 'hashed-password',
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    jwtService = {
      signAsync: jest.fn(),
    };

    cryptoService = {
      hashPassword: jest.fn(),
      comparePassword: jest.fn(),
    };

    transactionService = {
      runInTransaction: jest.fn(),
    };

    organizationService = {
      create: jest.fn(),
    };

    usersService = {
      createOwner: jest.fn(),
      findByEmail: jest.fn(),
      findAuthUserById: jest.fn(),
    };

    rbacService = {
      findRoleById: jest.fn(),
    };

    service = new AuthService(
      jwtService as unknown as JwtService,
      cryptoService as unknown as CryptoService,
      transactionService as unknown as TransactionService,
      organizationService as unknown as OrganizationService,
      usersService as unknown as UsersService,
      rbacService as unknown as RbacService,
    );
  });

  describe('register', () => {
    it('should register an organization and its owner successfully', async () => {
      const dto = {
        organizationName: 'Branchwork',
        name: 'John Owner',
        email: 'owner@example.com',
        password: 'StrongPassword123!',
      };

      const organization = {
        id: organizationId,
        name: dto.organizationName,
      };

      const passwordHash = 'hashed-password';
      const accessToken = 'generated-access-token';

      cryptoService.hashPassword.mockResolvedValue(passwordHash);

      organizationService.create.mockResolvedValue(
        organization as any,
      );

      usersService.createOwner.mockResolvedValue(
        owner as any,
      );

      transactionService.runInTransaction.mockImplementation(
        async (callback) => callback(manager),
      );

      jwtService.signAsync.mockResolvedValue(accessToken);

      const result = await service.register(dto as any);

      expect(cryptoService.hashPassword).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        dto.password,
      );

      expect(transactionService.runInTransaction).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(
        1,
      );

      expect(organizationService.create).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        dto.organizationName,
        manager,
      );

      expect(usersService.createOwner).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        dto.name,
        dto.email,
        passwordHash,
        manager,
      );

      expect(jwtService.signAsync).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        sub: userId,
      });

      expect(result).toEqual({
        accessToken,
        user: {
          id: userId,
          organizationId,
          name: owner.name,
          email: owner.email,
          userType: 'OWNER',
        },
      });

      expect(result.user).not.toHaveProperty('passwordHash');
    });

    it('should not create an organization or owner when password hashing fails', async () => {
      const error = new Error('Password hashing failed.');

      cryptoService.hashPassword.mockRejectedValue(error);

      await expect(
        service.register({
          organizationName: 'Branchwork',
          name: 'John Owner',
          email: 'owner@example.com',
          password: 'StrongPassword123!',
        } as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(error);

      expect(transactionService.runInTransaction).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(organizationService.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(usersService.createOwner).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should not generate a token when the transaction fails', async () => {
      const error = new Error('Transaction failed.');

      cryptoService.hashPassword.mockResolvedValue(
        'hashed-password',
      );

      transactionService.runInTransaction.mockRejectedValue(
        error,
      );

      await expect(
        service.register({
          organizationName: 'Branchwork',
          name: 'John Owner',
          email: 'owner@example.com',
          password: 'StrongPassword123!',
        } as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(error);

      expect(jwtService.signAsync).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });
  });

  describe('login', () => {
    it('should log in successfully with valid credentials', async () => {
      usersService.findByEmail.mockResolvedValue(
        owner as any,
      );

      cryptoService.comparePassword.mockResolvedValue(true);

      jwtService.signAsync.mockResolvedValue(
        'generated-access-token',
      );

      const result = await service.login({
        email: owner.email,
        password: 'StrongPassword123!',
      } as any);

      expect(usersService.findByEmail).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        owner.email,
      );

      expect(cryptoService.comparePassword).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'StrongPassword123!',
        owner.passwordHash,
      );

      expect(jwtService.signAsync).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
        sub: userId,
      });

      expect(result).toEqual({
        accessToken: 'generated-access-token',
        user: {
          id: userId,
          organizationId,
          name: owner.name,
          email: owner.email,
          userType: 'OWNER',
        },
      });

      expect(result.user).not.toHaveProperty('passwordHash');
    });

    it('should reject an unknown email', async () => {
      usersService.findByEmail.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(
        service.login({
          email: 'unknown@example.com',
          password: 'StrongPassword123!',
        } as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new UnauthorizedException(
          'Invalid email or password.',
        ),
      );

      expect(cryptoService.comparePassword).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(jwtService.signAsync).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject a user without a stored password hash', async () => {
      usersService.findByEmail.mockResolvedValue({
        ...owner,
        passwordHash: null,
      } as any);

      await expect(
        service.login({
          email: owner.email,
          password: 'StrongPassword123!',
        } as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(UnauthorizedException);

      expect(cryptoService.comparePassword).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(jwtService.signAsync).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject an incorrect password', async () => {
      usersService.findByEmail.mockResolvedValue(
        owner as any,
      );

      cryptoService.comparePassword.mockResolvedValue(false);

      await expect(
        service.login({
          email: owner.email,
          password: 'WrongPassword123!',
        } as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new UnauthorizedException(
          'Invalid email or password.',
        ),
      );

      expect(jwtService.signAsync).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });
  });

  describe('getProfile', () => {
    it('should throw UnauthorizedException when the user does not exist', async () => {
      usersService.findAuthUserById.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(
        service.getProfile(userId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new UnauthorizedException(
          'Invalid or expired access token.',
        ),
      );

      expect(rbacService.findRoleById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should return the complete permission catalog for an owner', async () => {
      usersService.findAuthUserById.mockResolvedValue(
        owner as any,
      );

      const result = await service.getProfile(userId);

      expect(result).toEqual({
        id: userId,
        organizationId,
        userType: 'OWNER',
        name: owner.name,
        branchId: null,
        roleName: null,
        permissions: [...PERMISSION_CATALOG],
      });

      expect(rbacService.findRoleById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should throw ForbiddenException when staff has no assigned role', async () => {
      usersService.findAuthUserById.mockResolvedValue({
        ...staff,
        roleId: null,
      } as any);

      await expect(
        service.getProfile(staff.id),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new ForbiddenException(
          'Your account does not have an assigned role.',
        ),
      );

      expect(rbacService.findRoleById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should return the role and permissions for staff', async () => {
      const permissions = [
        'product.view',
        'product.organize',
      ];

      usersService.findAuthUserById.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      rbacService.findRoleById.mockResolvedValue({
        id: roleId,
        organizationId,
        name: 'Branch Manager',
        permissions,
      } as any);

      const result = await service.getProfile(staff.id);

      expect(rbacService.findRoleById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        roleId,
      );

      expect(result).toEqual({
        id: staff.id,
        organizationId,
        userType: 'STAFF',
        name: staff.name,
        branchId: staff.branchId,
        roleName: 'Branch Manager',
        permissions,
      });
    });
  });
});

import {
  Injectable,
  UnauthorizedException,
  ForbiddenException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { JwtService } from '@nestjs/jwt';
import { CryptoService } from '../../common/services/crypto.service';
import { TransactionService } from '../../common/services/transaction.service';
import { PERMISSION_CATALOG } from '../../common/constants/permissions.constants';

import { OrganizationService } from '../organization/organization.service';
import { UsersService } from '../users/users.service';
import { RbacService } from './rbac.service';

import { RegisterDto } from './dto/register.dto';
import { LoginDto } from './dto/login.dto';

@Injectable()
export class AuthService {
  constructor(
    private readonly jwtService: JwtService,
    private readonly cryptoService: CryptoService,
    private readonly transactionService: TransactionService,
    private readonly organizationService: OrganizationService,
    private readonly usersService: UsersService,
    private readonly rbacService: RbacService,
  ) {}

  async register(dto: RegisterDto) {
    const passwordHash = await this.cryptoService.hashPassword(
      dto.password,
    );

    // Create the organization and owner atomically.
    const result = await this.transactionService.runInTransaction(
      async (manager) => {
        const organization = await this.organizationService.create(
          dto.organizationName,
          manager,
        );

        const user = await this.usersService.createOwner(
          organization.id,
          dto.name,
          dto.email,
          passwordHash,
          manager,
        );

        return { organization, user };
      },
    );

    const accessToken = await this.generateToken(
      result.user.id,
    );

    return {
      accessToken,
      user: {
        id: result.user.id,
        organizationId: result.user.organizationId,
        name: result.user.name,
        email: result.user.email,
        userType: result.user.userType,
      },
    };
  }

  async login(dto: LoginDto) {
    const user = await this.usersService.findByEmail(
      dto.email,
    );

    // Use the same response for an unknown email and wrong password.
    if (
      !user ||
      !user.passwordHash ||
      !(await this.cryptoService.comparePassword(
        dto.password,
        user.passwordHash,
      ))
    ) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException(
        'Invalid email or password.',
      );
    }

    const accessToken = await this.generateToken(user.id);

    return {
      accessToken,
      user: {
        id: user.id,
        organizationId: user.organizationId,
        name: user.name,
        email: user.email,
        userType: user.userType,
      },
    };
  }

  async getProfile(userId: string) {
    const user = await this.usersService.findAuthUserById(
      userId,
    );

    if (!user) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException(
        'Invalid or expired access token.',
      );
    }

    // Owners have access to the complete permission catalog.
    if (user.userType === 'OWNER') {
      return {
        id: user.id,
        organizationId: user.organizationId,
        userType: user.userType,
        name: user.name,
        branchId: null,
        roleName: null,
        permissions: [...PERMISSION_CATALOG],
      };
    }

    if (!user.roleId) {
      throw new ForbiddenException(
        'Your account does not have an assigned role.',
      );
    }

    const role = await this.rbacService.findRoleById(
      user.organizationId,
      user.roleId,
    );

    return {
      id: user.id,
      organizationId: user.organizationId,
      userType: user.userType,
      name: user.name,
      branchId: user.branchId,
      roleName: role.name,
      permissions: role.permissions,
    };
  }

  private async generateToken(userId: string): Promise<string> {
    return this.jwtService.signAsync({
      sub: userId,
    });
  }
}

import {
  Body,
  Controller,
  Delete,
  Get,
  HttpCode,
  HttpStatus,
  Param,
  ParseUUIDPipe,
  Patch,
  Post,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  ApiBearerAuth,
  ApiCreatedResponse,
  ApiOkResponse,
  ApiOperation,
  ApiTags,
} from '@nestjs/swagger';

import { OwnerOnly } from './decorators/auth.decorator';
import { GetUser } from '../../common/request-metadata/getUser.decorator';
import type { RequestUser } from '../../common/interfaces/request-user.interface';

import { RbacAdminService } from './rbac-admin.service';
import { CreateRoleDto } from './dto/create-role.dto';
import { UpdateRoleDto } from './dto/update-role.dto';
import { RoleResponseDto } from './dto/role-response.dto';

@ApiTags('Roles')
@ApiBearerAuth()
@OwnerOnly()
@Controller('roles')
export class RbacAdminController {
  constructor(
    private readonly rbacAdminService: RbacAdminService,
  ) {}

  @Post()
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Create a role' })
  @ApiCreatedResponse({ type: RoleResponseDto })
  async createRole(
    @GetUser() user: RequestUser,
    @Body() dto: CreateRoleDto,
  ): Promise<RoleResponseDto> {
    return this.rbacAdminService.createRole(
      user.organizationId,
      dto,
    );
  }

  @Get('permissions')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get the available permission catalog' })
  @ApiOkResponse({ schema: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'string' } } })
  getPermissionCatalog(): string[] {
    return this.rbacAdminService.getPermissionCatalog();
  }

  @Get()
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get all organization roles' })
  @ApiOkResponse({ type: RoleResponseDto, isArray: true })
  async findAllRoles(
    @GetUser() user: RequestUser,
  ): Promise<RoleResponseDto[]> {
    return this.rbacAdminService.findAllRoles(user.organizationId);
  }

  @Get(':id')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get a role by ID' })
  @ApiOkResponse({ type: RoleResponseDto })
  async findRole(
    @GetUser() user: RequestUser,
    @Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) roleId: string,
  ): Promise<RoleResponseDto> {
    return this.rbacAdminService.findRole(
      user.organizationId,
      roleId,
    );
  }

  @Patch(':id')
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Update a role' })
  @ApiOkResponse({ type: RoleResponseDto })
  async updateRole(
    @GetUser() user: RequestUser,
    @Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) roleId: string,
    @Body() dto: UpdateRoleDto,
  ): Promise<RoleResponseDto> {
    return this.rbacAdminService.updateRole(
      user.organizationId,
      roleId,
      dto,
    );
  }

  @Delete(':id')
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Delete a role' })
  @ApiOkResponse({
    schema: {
      example: { message: 'Role deleted successfully.' },
    },
  })
  async deleteRole(
    @GetUser() user: RequestUser,
    @Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) roleId: string,
  ): Promise<{ message: string }> {
    await this.rbacAdminService.deleteRole(
      user.organizationId,
      roleId,
    );

    return { message: 'Role deleted successfully.' };
  }
}

import {
  BadRequestException,
  ConflictException,
  NotFoundException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { QueryFailedError } from 'typeorm';

import { PERMISSIONS } from '../../common/constants/permissions.constants';
import { Role } from '../../schemas/Role.schema';
import { RbacRepository } from './rbac.repository';
import { RbacAdminService } from './rbac-admin.service';

function createQueryFailedError(code: string): QueryFailedError {
  const driverError = Object.assign(
    new Error('Database query failed.'),
    { code },
  );

  return new QueryFailedError(
    'TEST QUERY',
    [],
    driverError,
  );
}

describe('RbacAdminService', () => {
  let service: RbacAdminService;
  let repository: jest.Mocked<
    Pick<
      RbacRepository,
      'create' | 'findAll' | 'findById' | 'update' | 'delete'
    >
  >;

  const organizationId = 'organization-123';
  const roleId = 'role-123';

  const role = {
    id: roleId,
    organizationId,
    name: 'Branch Manager',
    permissions: [PERMISSIONS.PRODUCT_VIEW],
  } as Role;

  beforeEach(() => {
    repository = {
      create: jest.fn(),
      findAll: jest.fn(),
      findById: jest.fn(),
      update: jest.fn(),
      delete: jest.fn(),
    };

    service = new RbacAdminService(
      repository as unknown as RbacRepository,
    );
  });

  describe('createRole', () => {
    it('should create a role with valid permissions', async () => {
      repository.create.mockResolvedValue(role);

      const dto = {
        name: 'Branch Manager',
        permissions: [PERMISSIONS.PRODUCT_VIEW],
      };

      const result = await service.createRole(
        organizationId,
        dto,
      );

      expect(repository.create).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        dto.name,
        dto.permissions,
      );
      expect(result).toEqual(role);
    });

    it('should allow creating a role with no permissions', async () => {
      const roleWithoutPermissions = {
        ...role,
        permissions: [],
      } as Role;

      repository.create.mockResolvedValue(
        roleWithoutPermissions,
      );

      const result = await service.createRole(
        organizationId,
        {
          name: 'New Role',
          permissions: [],
        },
      );

      expect(result.permissions).toEqual([]);
    });

    it('should reject product.organize without product.view', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.createRole(organizationId, {
          name: 'Invalid Role',
          permissions: [PERMISSIONS.PRODUCT_ORGANIZE],
        }),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new BadRequestException(
          'product.organize requires product.view.',
        ),
      );

      expect(repository.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should allow product.organize when product.view is also assigned', async () => {
      const permissions = [
        PERMISSIONS.PRODUCT_VIEW,
        PERMISSIONS.PRODUCT_ORGANIZE,
      ];

      repository.create.mockResolvedValue({
        ...role,
        permissions,
      } as Role);

      await service.createRole(organizationId, {
        name: 'Branch Manager',
        permissions,
      });

      expect(repository.create).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        'Branch Manager',
        permissions,
      );
    });

    it('should throw ConflictException when the role name already exists', async () => {
      repository.create.mockRejectedValue(
        createQueryFailedError('23505'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.createRole(organizationId, {
          name: 'Branch Manager',
          permissions: [],
        }),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new ConflictException(
          'A role with this name already exists.',
        ),
      );
    });

    it('should propagate errors that are not database query errors', async () => {
      const error = new Error('Unexpected error.');
      repository.create.mockRejectedValue(error);

      await expect(
        service.createRole(organizationId, {
          name: 'Branch Manager',
          permissions: [],
        }),
      ).rejects.toThrow(error);
    });
  });

  describe('findAllRoles', () => {
    it('should return all roles belonging to the organization', async () => {
      repository.findAll.mockResolvedValue([role]);

      const result = await service.findAllRoles(
        organizationId,
      );

      expect(repository.findAll).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
      );
      expect(result).toEqual([role]);
    });
  });

  describe('findRole', () => {
    it('should return the requested role', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(role);

      const result = await service.findRole(
        organizationId,
        roleId,
      );

      expect(repository.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        roleId,
      );
      expect(result).toEqual(role);
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException when the role does not exist', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(
        service.findRole(organizationId, roleId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new NotFoundException('Role not found.'),
      );
    });
  });

  describe('updateRole', () => {
    it('should update a role name', async () => {
      const dto = { name: 'Updated Manager' };
      const updatedRole = {
        ...role,
        name: dto.name,
      } as Role;

      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(role);
      repository.update.mockResolvedValue(updatedRole);

      const result = await service.updateRole(
        organizationId,
        roleId,
        dto,
      );

      expect(repository.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        roleId,
      );
      expect(repository.update).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        role,
        dto,
      );
      expect(result).toEqual(updatedRole);
    });

    it('should update role permissions', async () => {
      const dto = {
        permissions: [
          PERMISSIONS.PRODUCT_VIEW,
          PERMISSIONS.PRODUCT_ORGANIZE,
        ],
      };

      const updatedRole = {
        ...role,
        permissions: dto.permissions,
      } as Role;

      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(role);
      repository.update.mockResolvedValue(updatedRole);

      const result = await service.updateRole(
        organizationId,
        roleId,
        dto,
      );

      expect(repository.update).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        role,
        dto,
      );
      expect(result.permissions).toEqual(dto.permissions);
    });

    it('should reject an update without any fields', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.updateRole(organizationId, roleId, {}),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new BadRequestException(
          'At least one field must be provided.',
        ),
      );

      expect(repository.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(repository.update).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject product.organize without product.view during update', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.updateRole(organizationId, roleId, {
          permissions: [PERMISSIONS.PRODUCT_ORGANIZE],
        }),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new BadRequestException(
          'product.organize requires product.view.',
        ),
      );

      expect(repository.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(repository.update).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException when updating a nonexistent role', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(
        service.updateRole(organizationId, roleId, {
          name: 'Updated Manager',
        }),
      ).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);

      expect(repository.update).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should throw ConflictException when the updated role name already exists', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(role);
      repository.update.mockRejectedValue(
        createQueryFailedError('23505'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.updateRole(organizationId, roleId, {
          name: 'Existing Role',
        }),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new ConflictException(
          'A role with this name already exists.',
        ),
      );
    });
  });

  describe('deleteRole', () => {
    it('should delete an existing role', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(role);
      repository.delete.mockResolvedValue(undefined);

      await expect(
        service.deleteRole(organizationId, roleId),
      ).resolves.toBeUndefined();

      expect(repository.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        roleId,
      );
      expect(repository.delete).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        roleId,
      );
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException when deleting a nonexistent role', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(
        service.deleteRole(organizationId, roleId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);

      expect(repository.delete).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should throw ConflictException when the role is assigned to staff', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(role);
      repository.delete.mockRejectedValue(
        createQueryFailedError('23503'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.deleteRole(organizationId, roleId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new ConflictException(
          'This role is assigned to staff members and cannot be deleted.',
        ),
      );
    });
  });

  describe('getPermissionCatalog', () => {
    it('should return all available permissions', () => {
      expect(service.getPermissionCatalog()).toEqual(
        Object.values(PERMISSIONS),
      );
    });
  });
});

import {
  BadRequestException,
  ConflictException,
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { QueryFailedError } from 'typeorm';

import { RbacRepository } from './rbac.repository';
import { CreateRoleDto } from './dto/create-role.dto';
import { UpdateRoleDto } from './dto/update-role.dto';
import { PERMISSIONS } from '../../common/constants/permissions.constants';
import { Role } from '../../schemas/Role.schema';

interface PostgresError {
  code?: string;
}

@Injectable()
export class RbacAdminService {
  constructor(
    private readonly rbacRepository: RbacRepository,
  ) {}

  async createRole(
    organizationId: string,
    dto: CreateRoleDto,
  ): Promise<Role> {
    this.validatePermissions(dto.permissions);

    try {
      return await this.rbacRepository.create(
        organizationId,
        dto.name,
        dto.permissions,
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.handleDatabaseError(error);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  async findAllRoles(organizationId: string): Promise<Role[]> {
    return this.rbacRepository.findAll(organizationId);
  }

  async findRole(
    organizationId: string,
    roleId: string,
  ): Promise<Role> {
    const role = await this.rbacRepository.findById(
      organizationId,
      roleId,
    );

    if (!role) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Role not found.');
    }

    return role;
  }

  async updateRole(
    organizationId: string,
    roleId: string,
    dto: UpdateRoleDto,
  ): Promise<Role> {
    if (
      dto.name === undefined &&
      dto.permissions === undefined
    ) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        'At least one field must be provided.',
      );
    }

    if (dto.permissions !== undefined) {
      this.validatePermissions(dto.permissions);
    }

    const role = await this.findRole(
      organizationId,
      roleId,
    );

    try {
      return await this.rbacRepository.update(role, dto);
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.handleDatabaseError(error);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  async deleteRole(
    organizationId: string,
    roleId: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.findRole(organizationId, roleId);

    try {
      await this.rbacRepository.delete(
        organizationId,
        roleId,
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.handleDatabaseError(error);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  getPermissionCatalog(): string[] {
    return Object.values(PERMISSIONS);
  }

  private validatePermissions(permissions: string[]): void {
    if (
      permissions.includes(PERMISSIONS.PRODUCT_ORGANIZE) &&
      !permissions.includes(PERMISSIONS.PRODUCT_VIEW)
    ) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        'product.organize requires product.view.',
      );
    }
  }

  private handleDatabaseError(error: unknown): void {
    if (!(error instanceof QueryFailedError)) {
      return;
    }

    const databaseError =
      error.driverError as PostgresError;

    if (databaseError.code === '23505') {
      throw new ConflictException(
        'A role with this name already exists.',
      );
    }

    if (databaseError.code === '23503') {
      throw new ConflictException(
        'This role is assigned to staff members and cannot be deleted.',
      );
    }
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\modules\auth\rbac.repository.ts

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { Repository } from 'typeorm';

import { Role } from '../../schemas/Role.schema';

@Injectable()
export class RbacRepository {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(Role)
    private readonly repository: Repository<Role>,
  ) {}

  async create(
    organizationId: string,
    name: string,
    permissions: string[],
  ): Promise<Role> {
    const role = this.repository.create({
      organizationId,
      name,
      permissions,
    });

    return this.repository.save(role);
  }

  async findAll(organizationId: string): Promise<Role[]> {
    return this.repository.find({
      where: { organizationId },
      order: {
        createdAt: 'ASC',
        id: 'ASC',
      },
    });
  }

  async findById(
    organizationId: string,
    roleId: string,
  ): Promise<Role | null> {
    return this.repository.findOne({
      where: {
        id: roleId,
        organizationId,
      },
    });
  }

  async update(
    role: Role,
    updates: Partial<Pick<Role, 'name' | 'permissions'>>,
  ): Promise<Role> {
    Object.assign(role, updates);

    return this.repository.save(role);
  }

  async delete(
    organizationId: string,
    roleId: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.repository.delete({
      id: roleId,
      organizationId,
    });
  }
}

import { NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';

import { PERMISSION_CATALOG } from '../../common/constants/permissions.constants';
import { RbacRepository } from './rbac.repository';
import { RbacService } from './rbac.service';

describe('RbacService', () => {
  let service: RbacService;
  let repository: jest.Mocked<
    Pick<RbacRepository, 'findById'>
  >;

  beforeEach(() => {
    repository = {
      findById: jest.fn(),
    };

    service = new RbacService(
      repository as unknown as RbacRepository,
    );
  });

  describe('findRoleById', () => {
    it('should return a role belonging to the requested organization', async () => {
      const role = {
        id: 'role-123',
        organizationId: 'organization-123',
        name: 'Branch Manager',
        permissions: ['product.view'],
      };

      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(role as any);

      const result = await service.findRoleById(
        'organization-123',
        'role-123',
      );

      expect(repository.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'organization-123',
        'role-123',
      );
      expect(result).toEqual(role);
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException when the role does not exist', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(null as any);

      await expect(
        service.findRoleById(
          'organization-123',
          'missing-role',
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new NotFoundException('Role not found.'),
      );
    });
  });

  describe('getRolePermissions', () => {
    it('should return the permissions assigned to the role', async () => {
      const permissions = [
        'product.view',
        'product.organize',
      ];

      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue({
        id: 'role-123',
        permissions,
      } as any);

      const result = await service.getRolePermissions(
        'organization-123',
        'role-123',
      );

      expect(result).toEqual(permissions);
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException when the role does not exist', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(null as any);

      await expect(
        service.getRolePermissions(
          'organization-123',
          'missing-role',
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);
    });
  });

  describe('getPermissionCatalog', () => {
    it('should return the complete permission catalog', () => {
      const result = service.getPermissionCatalog();

      expect(result).toEqual(PERMISSION_CATALOG);
    });

    it('should return a separate array instead of exposing the original catalog', () => {
      const result = service.getPermissionCatalog();

      expect(result).not.toBe(PERMISSION_CATALOG);
    });
  });
});

import { Injectable, NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';

import { RbacRepository } from './rbac.repository';
import { Role } from '../../schemas/Role.schema';
import { PERMISSION_CATALOG } from '../../common/constants/permissions.constants';

@Injectable()
export class RbacService {
  constructor(
    private readonly rbacRepository: RbacRepository,
  ) {}

  async findRoleById(
    organizationId: string,
    roleId: string,
  ): Promise<Role> {
    const role = await this.rbacRepository.findById(
      organizationId,
      roleId,
    );

    if (!role) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Role not found.');
    }

    return role;
  }

  async getRolePermissions(
    organizationId: string,
    roleId: string,
  ): Promise<string[]> {
    const role = await this.findRoleById(organizationId, roleId);

    return role.permissions;
  }

  getPermissionCatalog(): string[] {
    return [...PERMISSION_CATALOG];
  }
}

import { SetMetadata } from '@nestjs/common';

export const ACCESS_METADATA_KEY = 'access';

export type AccessType =
  | 'PUBLIC'
  | 'AUTHENTICATED'
  | 'OWNER_ONLY'
  | 'PERMISSIONS';

export const Public = () =>
  SetMetadata(ACCESS_METADATA_KEY, 'PUBLIC');

export const Authenticated = () =>
  SetMetadata(ACCESS_METADATA_KEY, 'AUTHENTICATED');

export const OwnerOnly = () =>
  SetMetadata(ACCESS_METADATA_KEY, 'OWNER_ONLY');

import {
  applyDecorators,
  SetMetadata,
} from '@nestjs/common';

import type { Permission } from '../../../common/constants/permissions.constants';
import { ACCESS_METADATA_KEY } from './auth.decorator';

export const REQUIRED_PERMISSIONS_KEY = 'required_permissions';

export const RequirePermissions = (
  ...permissions: Permission[]
) =>
  applyDecorators(
    SetMetadata(ACCESS_METADATA_KEY, 'PERMISSIONS'),
    SetMetadata(REQUIRED_PERMISSIONS_KEY, permissions),
  );

  ArrayUnique,
  IsArray,
  IsIn,
  IsString,
  Length,
} from 'class-validator';
import { ApiProperty } from '@nestjs/swagger';

import { PERMISSION_CATALOG } from '../../../common/constants/permissions.constants';
import { NoLeadingSpecialChars } from '../../../common/decorators/noLeadingSpecialChars';

export class CreateRoleDto {
  @ApiProperty({
    example: 'Branch Manager',
    description: 'Name of the role',
  })
  @Transform(({ value }) =>
    typeof value === 'string' ? value.trim() : value,
  )
  @IsString()
  @Length(1, 100)
  @NoLeadingSpecialChars()
  name!: string;

  @ApiProperty({
    example: ['product.view', 'product.organize', 'branch.view'],
    description: 'Permissions assigned to this role',
    isArray: true,
    enum: PERMISSION_CATALOG,
  })
  @IsArray()
  @ArrayUnique()
  @IsIn(PERMISSION_CATALOG, { each: true })
  permissions!: string[];
}

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\modules\auth\guards\access.guard.ts
==================================================

import {
  CanActivate,
  ExecutionContext,
  ForbiddenException,
  Injectable,
  UnauthorizedException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';
import type { Request } from 'express';

import type { RequestUser } from '../../../common/interfaces/request-user.interface';
import type { Permission } from '../../../common/constants/permissions.constants';

import { RbacService } from '../rbac.service';
import { ACCESS_METADATA_KEY } from '../decorators/auth.decorator';
import { REQUIRED_PERMISSIONS_KEY } from '../decorators/permissions.decorator';

interface AuthenticatedRequest extends Request {
  user?: RequestUser;
}

@Injectable()
export class AccessGuard implements CanActivate {
  constructor(
    private readonly reflector: Reflector,
    private readonly rbacService: RbacService,
  ) {}

  async canActivate(
    context: ExecutionContext,
  ): Promise<boolean> {
    const accessType = this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<string>(
      ACCESS_METADATA_KEY,
      [
        context.getHandler(),
        context.getClass(),
      ],
    );

    if (accessType === 'PUBLIC') {
      return true;
    }

    const request =
      context.switchToHttp().getRequest<AuthenticatedRequest>();

    const user = request.user;

    if (!user) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException(
        'Authentication is required.',
      );
    }

    if (accessType === 'OWNER_ONLY') {
      if (user.userType !== 'OWNER') {
        throw new ForbiddenException(
          'Only organization owners can perform this action.',
        );
      }

      return true;
    }

    if (accessType !== 'PERMISSIONS') {
      return true;
    }

    // Owners bypass permission checks.
    if (user.userType === 'OWNER') {
      return true;
    }

    const requiredPermissions =
      this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<Permission[]>(
        REQUIRED_PERMISSIONS_KEY,
        [
          context.getHandler(),
          context.getClass(),
        ],
      ) ?? [];

    if (requiredPermissions.length === 0) {
      return true;
    }

    if (!user.roleId) {
      throw new ForbiddenException(
        'Your account does not have an assigned role.',
      );
    }

    const grantedPermissions =
      await this.rbacService.getRolePermissions(
        user.organizationId,
        user.roleId,
      );

    const hasAllPermissions = requiredPermissions.every(
      (permission) =>
        grantedPermissions.includes(permission),
    );

    if (!hasAllPermissions) {
      throw new ForbiddenException(
        'You do not have permission to perform this action.',
      );
    }

    return true;
  }
}

import {
  ExecutionContext,
  Injectable,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { Reflector } from '@nestjs/core';
import { AuthGuard } from '@nestjs/passport';

import { ACCESS_METADATA_KEY } from '../decorators/auth.decorator';

@Injectable()
export class JwtAuthGuard extends AuthGuard('jwt') {
  constructor(
    private readonly reflector: Reflector,
  ) {
    super();
  }

  canActivate(context: ExecutionContext) {
    const accessType = this.reflector.getAllAndOverride<string>(
      ACCESS_METADATA_KEY,
      [
        context.getHandler(),
        context.getClass(),
      ],
    );

    if (accessType === 'PUBLIC') {
      return true;
    }

    return super.canActivate(context);
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\modules\auth\strategies\jwt.strategy.ts
==================================================

import {
  Injectable,
  UnauthorizedException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';
import { PassportStrategy } from '@nestjs/passport';
import {
  ExtractJwt,
  Strategy,
} from 'passport-jwt';

import { UsersService } from '../../users/users.service';
import type { JwtPayload } from '../../../common/interfaces/jwt-payload.interface';
import type { RequestUser } from '../../../common/interfaces/request-user.interface';

@Injectable()
export class JwtStrategy extends PassportStrategy(Strategy) {
  constructor(
    configService: ConfigService,
    private readonly usersService: UsersService,
  ) {
    super({
      jwtFromRequest: ExtractJwt.fromAuthHeaderAsBearerToken(),
      ignoreExpiration: false,
      secretOrKey: configService.getOrThrow<string>(
        'jwt.secret',
      ),
    });
  }

  async validate(payload: JwtPayload): Promise<RequestUser> {
    if (
      !payload ||
      typeof payload.sub !== 'string' ||
      !payload.sub.trim()
    ) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException(
        'Invalid or expired access token.',
      );
    }

    const user = await this.usersService.findAuthUserById(
      payload.sub,
    );

    if (!user) {
      throw new UnauthorizedException(
        'Invalid or expired access token.',
      );
    }

    return {
      id: user.id,
      organizationId: user.organizationId,
      userType: user.userType,
      branchId: user.branchId,
      roleId: user.roleId,
    };
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\modules\branches\branches.controller.ts
==================================================

import {
  Body,
  Controller,
  Delete,
  Get,
  HttpCode,
  HttpStatus,
  Param,
  ParseUUIDPipe,
  Patch,
  Post,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  ApiBearerAuth,
  ApiOperation,
  ApiResponse,
  ApiTags,
} from '@nestjs/swagger';

import { GetUser } from '../../common/request-metadata/getUser.decorator';
import type { RequestUser } from '../../common/interfaces/request-user.interface';
import { PERMISSIONS } from '../../common/constants/permissions.constants';

import { OwnerOnly } from '../auth/decorators/auth.decorator';
import { RequirePermissions } from '../auth/decorators/permissions.decorator';

import { BranchesService } from './branches.service';
import { CreateBranchDto } from './dto/create-branch.dto';
import { UpdateBranchDto } from './dto/update-branch.dto';
import { BranchResponseDto } from './dto/branch-response.dto';

@ApiTags('Branches')
@ApiBearerAuth()
@Controller('branches')
export class BranchesController {
  constructor(
    private readonly branchesService: BranchesService,
  ) {}

  @Post()
  @OwnerOnly()
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Create a branch' })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 201,
    description: 'Branch created successfully.',
    type: BranchResponseDto,
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Authentication required.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: 'Owner access required.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 409, description: 'Branch name already exists.' })
  async create(
    @GetUser() user: RequestUser,
    @Body() dto: CreateBranchDto,
  ): Promise<BranchResponseDto> {
    const branch = await this.branchesService.create(
      user.organizationId,
      dto,
    );

    return {
      id: branch.id,
      name: branch.name,
      organizationId: branch.organizationId,
      createdAt: branch.createdAt,
      updatedAt: branch.updatedAt,
    };
  }

  @Get()
  @RequirePermissions(PERMISSIONS.BRANCH_VIEW)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get all branches in the organization' })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: 'Branches retrieved successfully.',
    type: [BranchResponseDto],
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Authentication required.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: 'Insufficient permissions.' })
  async findAll(
    @GetUser() user: RequestUser,
  ): Promise<BranchResponseDto[]> {
    const branches = await this.branchesService.findAll(
      user.organizationId,
    );

    return branches.map((branch) => ({
      id: branch.id,
      name: branch.name,
      organizationId: branch.organizationId,
      createdAt: branch.createdAt,
      updatedAt: branch.updatedAt,
    }));
  }

  @Get(':id')
  @RequirePermissions(PERMISSIONS.BRANCH_VIEW)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get a branch by ID' })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: 'Branch retrieved successfully.',
    type: BranchResponseDto,
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Authentication required.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: 'Insufficient permissions.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Branch not found.' })
  async findById(
    @GetUser() user: RequestUser,
    @Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) branchId: string,
  ): Promise<BranchResponseDto> {
    const branch = await this.branchesService.findById(
      user.organizationId,
      branchId,
    );

    return {
      id: branch.id,
      name: branch.name,
      organizationId: branch.organizationId,
      createdAt: branch.createdAt,
      updatedAt: branch.updatedAt,
    };
  }

  @Patch(':id')
  @OwnerOnly()
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Update a branch' })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: 'Branch updated successfully.',
    type: BranchResponseDto,
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: 'Invalid update request.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Authentication required.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: 'Owner access required.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Branch not found.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 409, description: 'Branch name already exists.' })
  async update(
    @GetUser() user: RequestUser,
    @Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) branchId: string,
    @Body() dto: UpdateBranchDto,
  ): Promise<BranchResponseDto> {
    const branch = await this.branchesService.update(
      user.organizationId,
      branchId,
      dto,
    );

    return {
      id: branch.id,
      name: branch.name,
      organizationId: branch.organizationId,
      createdAt: branch.createdAt,
      updatedAt: branch.updatedAt,
    };
  }

  @Delete(':id')
  @OwnerOnly()
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Delete a branch' })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: 'Branch deleted successfully.',
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Authentication required.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: 'Owner access required.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Branch not found.' })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 409,
    description: 'Branch is currently in use and cannot be deleted.',
  })
  async delete(
    @GetUser() user: RequestUser,
    @Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) branchId: string,
  ): Promise<{ message: string }> {
    await this.branchesService.delete(
      user.organizationId,
      branchId,
    );

    return {
      message: 'Branch has been deleted successfully.',
    };
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\modules\branches\branches.repository.ts
==================================================
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import {
  EntityManager,
  Repository,
} from 'typeorm';

import { Branch } from '../../schemas/Branch.schema';

@Injectable()
export class BranchesRepository {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(Branch)
    private readonly repository: Repository<Branch>,
  ) {}

  async create(
    organizationId: string,
    name: string,
    manager?: EntityManager,
  ): Promise<Branch> {
    const repository = manager
      ? manager.getRepository(Branch)
      : this.repository;

    const branch = repository.create({
      organizationId,
      name,
    });

    return repository.save(branch);
  }

  async findAll(organizationId: string): Promise<Branch[]> {
    return this.repository.find({
      where: { organizationId },
      order: {
        createdAt: 'ASC',
        id: 'ASC',
      },
    });
  }

  async findById(
    organizationId: string,
    branchId: string,
  ): Promise<Branch | null> {
    return this.repository.findOne({
      where: {
        id: branchId,
        organizationId,
      },
    });
  }

  async update(
    branch: Branch,
    name: string,
  ): Promise<Branch> {
    branch.name = name;
    return this.repository.save(branch);
  }

  async delete(
    organizationId: string,
    branchId: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.repository.delete({
      id: branchId,
      organizationId,
    });
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\modules\branches\branches.service.spec.ts

import {
  BadRequestException,
  ConflictException,
  NotFoundException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { QueryFailedError } from 'typeorm';

import { Branch } from '../../schemas/Branch.schema';
import { BranchesRepository } from './branches.repository';
import { BranchesService } from './branches.service';

function createQueryFailedError(code: string): QueryFailedError {
  const driverError = Object.assign(
    new Error('Database query failed.'),
    { code },
  );

  return new QueryFailedError(
    'TEST QUERY',
    [],
    driverError,
  );
}

describe('BranchesService', () => {
  let service: BranchesService;
  let repository: jest.Mocked<
    Pick<
      BranchesRepository,
      'create' | 'findAll' | 'findById' | 'update' | 'delete'
    >
  >;

  const organizationId = 'organization-123';
  const branchId = 'branch-123';

  const branch = {
    id: branchId,
    organizationId,
    name: 'Dhaka Branch',
  } as Branch;

  beforeEach(() => {
    repository = {
      create: jest.fn(),
      findAll: jest.fn(),
      findById: jest.fn(),
      update: jest.fn(),
      delete: jest.fn(),
    };

    service = new BranchesService(
      repository as unknown as BranchesRepository,
    );
  });

  describe('create', () => {
    it('should create a branch for the organization', async () => {
      repository.create.mockResolvedValue(branch);

      const result = await service.create(organizationId, {
        name: 'Dhaka Branch',
      });

      expect(repository.create).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        'Dhaka Branch',
      );
      expect(result).toEqual(branch);
    });

    it('should throw ConflictException when a branch with the same name already exists', async () => {
      repository.create.mockRejectedValue(
        createQueryFailedError('23505'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.create(organizationId, {
          name: 'Dhaka Branch',
        }),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new ConflictException(
          'A branch with this name already exists.',
        ),
      );
    });

    it('should propagate errors that are not database query errors', async () => {
      const error = new Error('Unexpected error.');
      repository.create.mockRejectedValue(error);

      await expect(
        service.create(organizationId, {
          name: 'Dhaka Branch',
        }),
      ).rejects.toThrow(error);
    });
  });

  describe('findAll', () => {
    it('should return all branches belonging to the organization', async () => {
      repository.findAll.mockResolvedValue([branch]);

      const result = await service.findAll(organizationId);

      expect(repository.findAll).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
      );
      expect(result).toEqual([branch]);
    });
  });

  describe('findById', () => {
    it('should return a branch belonging to the organization', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(branch);

      const result = await service.findById(
        organizationId,
        branchId,
      );

      expect(repository.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        branchId,
      );
      expect(result).toEqual(branch);
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException when the branch does not exist', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(
        service.findById(organizationId, branchId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new NotFoundException('Branch not found.'),
      );
    });
  });

  describe('update', () => {
    it('should update the branch name', async () => {
      const updatedBranch = {
        ...branch,
        name: 'Updated Branch',
      } as Branch;

      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(branch);
      repository.update.mockResolvedValue(updatedBranch);

      const result = await service.update(
        organizationId,
        branchId,
        { name: 'Updated Branch' },
      );

      expect(repository.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        branchId,
      );
      expect(repository.update).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        branch,
        'Updated Branch',
      );
      expect(result).toEqual(updatedBranch);
    });

    it('should reject an update without a name', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.update(organizationId, branchId, {}),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new BadRequestException(
          'At least one field must be provided.',
        ),
      );

      expect(repository.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(repository.update).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException when updating a nonexistent branch', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(
        service.update(organizationId, branchId, {
          name: 'Updated Branch',
        }),
      ).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);

      expect(repository.update).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should throw ConflictException when the updated name already exists', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(branch);
      repository.update.mockRejectedValue(
        createQueryFailedError('23505'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.update(organizationId, branchId, {
          name: 'Existing Branch',
        }),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new ConflictException(
          'A branch with this name already exists.',
        ),
      );
    });
  });

  describe('delete', () => {
    it('should delete an existing branch', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(branch);
      repository.delete.mockResolvedValue(undefined);

      await expect(
        service.delete(organizationId, branchId),
      ).resolves.toBeUndefined();

      expect(repository.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        branchId,
      );
      expect(repository.delete).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        branchId,
      );
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException when deleting a nonexistent branch', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(
        service.delete(organizationId, branchId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);

      expect(repository.delete).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should throw ConflictException when the branch is still in use', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(branch);
      repository.delete.mockRejectedValue(
        createQueryFailedError('23503'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.delete(organizationId, branchId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new ConflictException(
          'This branch is currently in use and cannot be deleted.',
        ),
      );
    });
  });
});

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\modules\branches\branches.service.ts
==================================================

import {
  BadRequestException,
  ConflictException,
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { QueryFailedError } from 'typeorm';

import { Branch } from '../../schemas/Branch.schema';
import { CreateBranchDto } from './dto/create-branch.dto';
import { UpdateBranchDto } from './dto/update-branch.dto';
import { BranchesRepository } from './branches.repository';

interface PostgresError {
  code?: string;
  constraint?: string;
}

@Injectable()
export class BranchesService {
  constructor(
    private readonly branchesRepository: BranchesRepository,
  ) {}

  async create(
    organizationId: string,
    dto: CreateBranchDto,
  ): Promise<Branch> {
    try {
      return await this.branchesRepository.create(
        organizationId,
        dto.name,
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.handleDatabaseError(error);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  async findAll(organizationId: string): Promise<Branch[]> {
    return this.branchesRepository.findAll(organizationId);
  }

  async findById(
    organizationId: string,
    branchId: string,
  ): Promise<Branch> {
    const branch = await this.branchesRepository.findById(
      organizationId,
      branchId,
    );

    if (!branch) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Branch not found.');
    }

    return branch;
  }

  async update(
    organizationId: string,
    branchId: string,
    dto: UpdateBranchDto,
  ): Promise<Branch> {
    if (dto.name === undefined) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        'At least one field must be provided.',
      );
    }

    const branch = await this.findById(
      organizationId,
      branchId,
    );

    try {
      return await this.branchesRepository.update(
        branch,
        dto.name,
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.handleDatabaseError(error);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  async delete(
    organizationId: string,
    branchId: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.findById(organizationId, branchId);

    try {
      await this.branchesRepository.delete(
        organizationId,
        branchId,
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.handleDatabaseError(error);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  private handleDatabaseError(error: unknown): void {
    if (!(error instanceof QueryFailedError)) {
      return;
    }

    const databaseError =
      error.driverError as PostgresError;

    if (databaseError.code === '23505') {
      throw new ConflictException(
        'A branch with this name already exists.',
      );
    }

    if (databaseError.code === '23503') {
      throw new ConflictException(
        'This branch is currently in use and cannot be deleted.',
      );
    }
  }
}

  IsString,
  Length,
} from 'class-validator';

import { NoLeadingSpecialChars } from '../../../common/decorators/noLeadingSpecialChars';

export class CreateBranchDto {
  @ApiProperty({
    example: 'Dhanmondi Branch',
    minLength: 1,
    maxLength: 100,
  })
  @Transform(({ value }) =>
    typeof value === 'string' ? value.trim() : value,
  )

  @Length(1, 100)
  @NoLeadingSpecialChars()

import { ApiPropertyOptional } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { Transform } from 'class-transformer';
import {
  IsOptional,
  IsString,
  Length,
} from 'class-validator';

import { NoLeadingSpecialChars } from '../../../common/decorators/noLeadingSpecialChars';

    example: 'Gulshan Branch',
    minLength: 1,
    maxLength: 100,

  @IsOptional()
  @Transform(({ value }) =>
    typeof value === 'string' ? value.trim() : value,

import { OrganizationService } from './organization.service';

@ApiTags('Organization')
@ApiBearerAuth()
@Controller('organization')
export class OrganizationController {
  constructor(
    private readonly organizationService: OrganizationService,
  ) {}

  @Get('me')
  @Authenticated()
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: 'Get the current organization',
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 200,
    description: 'Organization retrieved successfully',
    type: OrganizationResponseDto,
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 401,
    description: 'Authentication required',
  })
  @ApiResponse({
    status: 404,
    description: 'Organization not found',
  })
  async findCurrent(
    @GetUser() user: RequestUser,
  ): Promise<OrganizationResponseDto> {
    const organization =
      await this.organizationService.findById(user.organizationId);

    return {
      id: organization.id,
      name: organization.name,
      createdAt: organization.createdAt,
      updatedAt: organization.updatedAt,
    };
  }
}

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import {
  EntityManager,
  Repository,
} from 'typeorm';

import { Organization } from '../../schemas/Organization.schema';

@Injectable()
export class OrganizationRepository {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(Organization)
    private readonly repository: Repository<Organization>,
  ) {}

  async create(
    name: string,
    manager?: EntityManager,
  ): Promise<Organization> {
    const repository = manager
      ? manager.getRepository(Organization)
      : this.repository;

    const organization = repository.create({ name });

    return repository.save(organization);
  }

  async findById(
    organizationId: string,
  ): Promise<Organization | null> {
    return this.repository.findOne({
      where: { id: organizationId },
    });
  }
}

import {
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import type { EntityManager } from 'typeorm';

import { Organization } from '../../schemas/Organization.schema';
import { OrganizationRepository } from './organization.repository';

@Injectable()
export class OrganizationService {
  constructor(
    private readonly organizationRepository: OrganizationRepository,
  ) {}

  async create(
    name: string,
    manager?: EntityManager,
  ): Promise<Organization> {
    return this.organizationRepository.create(name, manager);
  }

  async findById(
    organizationId: string,
  ): Promise<Organization> {
    const organization =
      await this.organizationRepository.findById(organizationId);

    if (!organization) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Organization not found.');
    }

    return organization;
  }
}
