import { NotFoundException } from '@nestjs/common';

import { OrganizationRepository } from './organization.repository';
import { OrganizationService } from './organization.service';

describe('OrganizationService', () => {
  let service: OrganizationService;
  let repository: jest.Mocked<
    Pick<OrganizationRepository, 'create' | 'findById'>
  >;

  beforeEach(() => {
    repository = {
      create: jest.fn(),
      findById: jest.fn(),
    };

    service = new OrganizationService(
      repository as unknown as OrganizationRepository,
    );
  });

  describe('create', () => {
    it('should delegate organization creation to the repository', async () => {
      const organization = {
        id: 'organization-123',
        name: 'Branchwork',
      };

      repository.create.mockResolvedValue(organization as any);

      const manager = {} as any;

      const result = await service.create(
        'Branchwork',
        manager,
      );

      expect(repository.create).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'Branchwork',
        manager,
      );
      expect(result).toEqual(organization);
    });

    it('should allow creation without an EntityManager', async () => {
      const organization = {
        id: 'organization-123',
        name: 'Branchwork',
      };

      repository.create.mockResolvedValue(organization as any);

      await service.create('Branchwork');

      expect(repository.create).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'Branchwork',
        undefined,
      );
    });
  });

  describe('findById', () => {
    it('should return the organization when it exists', async () => {
      const organization = {
        id: 'organization-123',
        name: 'Branchwork',
      };

      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(organization as any);

      const result = await service.findById('organization-123');

      expect(repository.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        'organization-123',
      );
      expect(result).toEqual(organization);
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException when the organization does not exist', async () => {
      repository.findById.mockResolvedValue(null as any);

      await expect(
        service.findById('organization-123'),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new NotFoundException('Organization not found.'),
      );
    });
  });
});

import {
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import type { EntityManager } from 'typeorm';

import { Organization } from '../../schemas/Organization.schema';
import { OrganizationRepository } from './organization.repository';

@Injectable()
export class OrganizationService {
  constructor(
    private readonly organizationRepository: OrganizationRepository,
  ) {}

  async create(
    name: string,
    manager?: EntityManager,
  ): Promise<Organization> {
    return this.organizationRepository.create(name, manager);
  }

  async findById(
    organizationId: string,
  ): Promise<Organization> {
    const organization =
      await this.organizationRepository.findById(organizationId);

    if (!organization) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Organization not found.');
    }

    return organization;
  }
}

import {
  Body,
  Controller,
  Delete,
  Get,
  HttpCode,
  HttpStatus,
  Param,
  ParseUUIDPipe,
  Patch,
  Post,
  Query,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  ApiBearerAuth,
  ApiOperation,
  ApiParam,
  ApiQuery,
  ApiResponse,
  ApiTags,
} from '@nestjs/swagger';

import { GetUser } from '../../common/request-metadata/getUser.decorator';
import type { RequestUser } from '../../common/interfaces/request-user.interface';
import type { PaginatedResult } from '../../common/interfaces/pagination.interface';
import { PERMISSIONS } from '../../common/constants/permissions.constants';

import { OwnerOnly } from '../auth/decorators/auth.decorator';
import { RequirePermissions } from '../auth/decorators/permissions.decorator';

import { Product } from '../../schemas/Product.schema';
import { ProductsService } from './products.service';

import { CreateProductDto } from './dto/create-product.dto';
import { OrganizeProductDto } from './dto/organize-product.dto';
import { ProductQueryDto } from './dto/product-query.dto';
import { ProductResponseDto } from './dto/product-response.dto';

@ApiTags('Products')
@ApiBearerAuth()
@Controller('products')
export class ProductsController {
  constructor(
    private readonly productsService: ProductsService,
  ) {}

  @Post()
  @OwnerOnly()
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: 'Create a product',
    description: 'OWNER only. The branch must belong to the authenticated organization.',
  })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 201, description: 'Product created successfully.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: 'Invalid product data.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Branch not found.' })
  async create(
    @GetUser() requester: RequestUser,
    @Body() dto: CreateProductDto,
  ): Promise<ProductResponseDto> {
    const product = await this.productsService.create(
      requester.organizationId,
      dto,
    );

    return this.toResponse(product);
  }

  @Get()
  @RequirePermissions(PERMISSIONS.PRODUCT_VIEW)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: 'Get paginated products',
    description: 'OWNER can view all organization products and optionally filter by branch. STAFF can only view products in their assigned branch.',
  })
  @ApiQuery({ name: 'page', required: false, type: Number })
  @ApiQuery({ name: 'limit', required: false, type: Number })
  @ApiQuery({ name: 'branchId', required: false, type: String })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Products retrieved successfully.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: 'Missing required permission.' })
  async findAll(
    @GetUser() requester: RequestUser,
    @Query() query: ProductQueryDto,
  ): Promise<PaginatedResult<ProductResponseDto>> {
    const result = await this.productsService.findAll(
      requester,
      {
        page: query.page,
        limit: query.limit,
      },
      query.branchId,
    );

    return {
      items: result.items.map((product) =>
        this.toResponse(product),
      ),
      meta: result.meta,
    };
  }

  @Get(':id')
  @RequirePermissions(PERMISSIONS.PRODUCT_VIEW)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: 'Get a product by ID',
    description: 'STAFF can only access products belonging to their assigned branch.',
  })
  @ApiParam({ name: 'id', format: 'uuid' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Product retrieved successfully.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Product not found.' })
  async findById(
    @GetUser() requester: RequestUser,
    @Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) productId: string,
  ): Promise<ProductResponseDto> {
    const product = await this.productsService.findById(
      requester,
      productId,
    );

    return this.toResponse(product);
  }

  @Patch(':id/organize')
  @RequirePermissions(
    PERMISSIONS.PRODUCT_VIEW,
    PERMISSIONS.PRODUCT_ORGANIZE,
  )
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: 'Organize a product',
    description: 'Requires both product.view and product.organize. At least one of category, stock, or branchId must be provided.',
  })
  @ApiParam({ name: 'id', format: 'uuid' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Product organized successfully.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: 'Invalid update data.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Product or destination branch not found.' })
  async organize(
    @GetUser() requester: RequestUser,
    @Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) productId: string,
    @Body() dto: OrganizeProductDto,
  ): Promise<ProductResponseDto> {
    const product = await this.productsService.organize(
      requester,
      productId,
      dto,
    );

    return this.toResponse(product);
  }

  @Delete(':id')
  @OwnerOnly()
  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
  @ApiOperation({
    summary: 'Delete a product',
    description: 'OWNER only.',
  })
  @ApiParam({ name: 'id', format: 'uuid' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Product deleted successfully.' })
  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Product not found.' })
  async delete(
    @GetUser() requester: RequestUser,
    @Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) productId: string,
  ): Promise<{ message: string }> {
    await this.productsService.delete(
      requester.organizationId,
      productId,
    );

    return {
      message: 'Product has been deleted successfully.',
    };
  }

  private toResponse(product: Product): ProductResponseDto {
    return {
      id: product.id,
      organizationId: product.organizationId,
      branchId: product.branchId,
      name: product.name,
      category: product.category,
      stock: product.stock,
      createdAt: product.createdAt,
      updatedAt: product.updatedAt,
    };
  }
}

import { Module } from '@nestjs/common';

@Module({
  imports: [
    TypeOrmModule.forFeature([Product]),
    BranchesModule,
  ],

  providers: [
    ProductsRepository,
    ProductsService,
  ],

==================================================
FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\modules\products\products.repository.ts
==================================================

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import { Repository } from 'typeorm';

import { Product } from '../../schemas/Product.schema';
import type { PaginationParams } from '../../common/interfaces/pagination.interface';

@Injectable()
export class ProductsRepository {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(Product)
    private readonly repository: Repository<Product>,
  ) {}

  async create(
    organizationId: string,
    branchId: string,
    name: string,
    category: string | null,
    stock: number,
  ): Promise<Product> {
    const product = this.repository.create({
      organizationId,
      branchId,
      name,
      category,
      stock,
    });

    return this.repository.save(product);
  }

  async findAll(
    organizationId: string,
    pagination: PaginationParams,
    branchId?: string,
  ): Promise<[Product[], number]> {
    const query = this.repository
      .createQueryBuilder('product')
      .where('product.organizationId = :organizationId', {
        organizationId,
      });

    if (branchId !== undefined) {
      query.andWhere('product.branchId = :branchId', {
        branchId,
      });
    }

    return query
      .orderBy('product.createdAt', 'ASC')
      .addOrderBy('product.id', 'ASC')
      .skip((pagination.page - 1) * pagination.limit)
      .take(pagination.limit)
      .getManyAndCount();
  }

  async findById(
    organizationId: string,
    productId: string,
    branchId?: string,
  ): Promise<Product | null> {
    const query = this.repository
      .createQueryBuilder('product')
      .where('product.id = :productId', {
        productId,
      })
      .andWhere('product.organizationId = :organizationId', {
        organizationId,
      });

    if (branchId !== undefined) {
      query.andWhere('product.branchId = :branchId', {
        branchId,
      });
    }

    return query.getOne();
  }

  async update(
    product: Product,
    updates: Partial<Pick<Product, 'category' | 'stock' | 'branchId'>>,
  ): Promise<Product> {
    Object.assign(product, updates);

    return this.repository.save(product);
  }

  async delete(
    organizationId: string,
    productId: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.repository.delete({
      id: productId,
      organizationId,
    });
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\modules\products\products.service.spec.ts
==================================================

import {
  BadRequestException,
  ConflictException,
  ForbiddenException,
  NotFoundException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { QueryFailedError } from 'typeorm';

import { BranchesService } from '../branches/branches.service';
import { ProductsRepository } from './products.repository';
import { ProductsService } from './products.service';

function createQueryFailedError(code: string): QueryFailedError {
  const driverError = Object.assign(
    new Error('Database query failed.'),
    { code },
  );

  return new QueryFailedError(
    'TEST QUERY',
    [],
    driverError,
  );
}

describe('ProductsService', () => {
  let service: ProductsService;

  let productsRepository: jest.Mocked<
    Pick<
      ProductsRepository,
      'create' | 'findAll' | 'findById' | 'update' | 'delete'
    >
  >;

  let branchesService: jest.Mocked<
    Pick<BranchesService, 'findById'>
  >;

  const organizationId = 'organization-123';
  const branchId = 'branch-123';
  const anotherBranchId = 'branch-456';
  const productId = 'product-123';

  const owner = {
    id: 'owner-123',
    organizationId,
    userType: 'OWNER',
    branchId: null,
    roleId: null,
  };

  const staff = {
    id: 'staff-123',
    organizationId,
    userType: 'STAFF',
    branchId,
    roleId: 'role-123',
  };

  const product = {
    id: productId,
    organizationId,
    branchId,
    name: 'Laptop',
    category: 'Electronics',
    stock: 10,
    createdAt: new Date('2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z'),
    updatedAt: new Date('2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z'),
  };

  const pagination = {
    page: 1,
    limit: 10,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    productsRepository = {
      create: jest.fn(),
      findAll: jest.fn(),
      findById: jest.fn(),
      update: jest.fn(),
      delete: jest.fn(),
    };

    branchesService = {
      findById: jest.fn(),
    };

    service = new ProductsService(
      productsRepository as unknown as ProductsRepository,
      branchesService as unknown as BranchesService,
    );
  });

  describe('create', () => {
    const dto = {
      name: 'Laptop',
      branchId,
      category: 'Electronics',
      stock: 10,
    };

    it('should create a product successfully', async () => {
      productsRepository.create.mockResolvedValue(
        product as any,
      );

      const result = await service.create(
        organizationId,
        dto as any,
      );

      expect(branchesService.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        branchId,
      );

      expect(productsRepository.create).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        branchId,
        dto.name,
        dto.category,
        dto.stock,
      );

      expect(result).toEqual(product);
    });

    it('should reject an empty organization ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.create('', dto as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new BadRequestException(
          'Organization ID is required.',
        ),
      );

      expect(productsRepository.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject an empty branch ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.create(organizationId, {
          ...dto,
          branchId: '',
        } as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new BadRequestException('Branch ID is required.'),
      );

      expect(branchesService.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(productsRepository.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should not create a product when the branch does not belong to the organization', async () => {
      branchesService.findById.mockRejectedValue(
        new NotFoundException('Branch not found.'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.create(organizationId, dto as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);

      expect(productsRepository.create).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should map a foreign key database error to ConflictException', async () => {
      productsRepository.create.mockRejectedValue(
        createQueryFailedError('23503'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.create(organizationId, dto as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(ConflictException);
    });

    it('should map a unique constraint database error to ConflictException', async () => {
      productsRepository.create.mockRejectedValue(
        createQueryFailedError('23505'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.create(organizationId, dto as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(ConflictException);
    });

    it('should map an invalid identifier database error to BadRequestException', async () => {
      productsRepository.create.mockRejectedValue(
        createQueryFailedError('22P02'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.create(organizationId, dto as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
    });

    it('should map an out-of-range numeric database error to BadRequestException', async () => {
      productsRepository.create.mockRejectedValue(
        createQueryFailedError('22003'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.create(organizationId, dto as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);
    });
  });

  describe('findAll', () => {
    it('should return products with pagination metadata for an owner', async () => {
      productsRepository.findAll.mockResolvedValue([
        [product as any],
        25,
      ]);

      const result = await service.findAll(
        owner as any,
        pagination as any,
      );

      expect(productsRepository.findAll).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        pagination,
        undefined,
      );

      expect(result).toEqual({
        items: [product],
        meta: {
          page: 1,
          limit: 10,
          total: 25,
          totalPages: 3,
        },
      });
    });

    it('should allow an owner to filter products by branch', async () => {
      productsRepository.findAll.mockResolvedValue([
        [product as any],
        1,
      ]);

      await service.findAll(
        owner as any,
        pagination as any,
        branchId,
      );

      expect(branchesService.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        branchId,
      );

      expect(productsRepository.findAll).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        pagination,
        branchId,
      );
    });

    it('should force staff to access only their assigned branch', async () => {
      productsRepository.findAll.mockResolvedValue([
        [product as any],
        1,
      ]);

      await service.findAll(
        staff as any,
        pagination as any,
        anotherBranchId,
      );

      expect(productsRepository.findAll).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        pagination,
        branchId,
      );

      expect(branchesService.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject staff without an assigned branch', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.findAll(
          {
            ...staff,
            branchId: null,
          } as any,
          pagination as any,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new ForbiddenException(
          'Your account is not assigned to a branch.',
        ),
      );

      expect(productsRepository.findAll).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject an empty organization ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.findAll(
          {
            ...owner,
            organizationId: '',
          } as any,
          pagination as any,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);

      expect(productsRepository.findAll).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should return an empty product list when no products exist', async () => {
      productsRepository.findAll.mockResolvedValue([
        [],
        0,
      ]);

      const result = await service.findAll(
        owner as any,
        pagination as any,
      );

      expect(result).toEqual({
        items: [],
        meta: {
          page: 1,
          limit: 10,
          total: 0,
          totalPages: 0,
        },
      });
    });
  });

  describe('findById', () => {
    it('should return a product for an owner', async () => {
      productsRepository.findById.mockResolvedValue(
        product as any,
      );

      const result = await service.findById(
        owner as any,
        productId,
      );

      expect(productsRepository.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        productId,
        undefined,
      );

      expect(result).toEqual(product);
    });

    it('should restrict staff product lookup to their assigned branch', async () => {
      productsRepository.findById.mockResolvedValue(
        product as any,
      );

      await service.findById(
        staff as any,
        productId,
      );

      expect(productsRepository.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        productId,
        branchId,
      );
    });

    it('should reject staff without an assigned branch', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.findById(
          {
            ...staff,
            branchId: null,
          } as any,
          productId,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);

      expect(productsRepository.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject an empty product ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.findById(owner as any, ''),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new BadRequestException('Product ID is required.'),
      );

      expect(productsRepository.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException when the product does not exist', async () => {
      productsRepository.findById.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(
        service.findById(owner as any, productId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new NotFoundException('Product not found.'),
      );
    });
  });

  describe('organize', () => {
    it('should update product category', async () => {
      const updatedProduct = {
        ...product,
        category: 'Computers',
      };

      productsRepository.findById.mockResolvedValue(
        product as any,
      );

      productsRepository.update.mockResolvedValue(
        updatedProduct as any,
      );

      const result = await service.organize(
        owner as any,
        productId,
        {
          category: 'Computers',
        } as any,
      );

      expect(productsRepository.update).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        product,
        {
          category: 'Computers',
        },
      );

      expect(result).toEqual(updatedProduct);
    });

    it('should update product stock', async () => {
      const updatedProduct = {
        ...product,
        stock: 20,
      };

      productsRepository.findById.mockResolvedValue(
        product as any,
      );

      productsRepository.update.mockResolvedValue(
        updatedProduct as any,
      );

      const result = await service.organize(
        owner as any,
        productId,
        {
          stock: 20,
        } as any,
      );

      expect(productsRepository.update).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        product,
        {
          stock: 20,
        },
      );

      expect(result.stock).toBe(20);
    });

    it('should move a product to another branch after validating the destination branch', async () => {
      const updatedProduct = {
        ...product,
        branchId: anotherBranchId,
      };

      productsRepository.findById.mockResolvedValue(
        product as any,
      );

      productsRepository.update.mockResolvedValue(
        updatedProduct as any,
      );

      await service.organize(
        owner as any,
        productId,
        {
          branchId: anotherBranchId,
        } as any,
      );

      expect(branchesService.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        anotherBranchId,
      );

      expect(productsRepository.update).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        product,
        {
          branchId: anotherBranchId,
        },
      );
    });

    it('should reject an empty update DTO', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.organize(
          owner as any,
          productId,
          {} as any,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new BadRequestException(
          'At least one field must be provided.',
        ),
      );

      expect(productsRepository.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(productsRepository.update).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should not update a product when the destination branch is invalid', async () => {
      productsRepository.findById.mockResolvedValue(
        product as any,
      );

      branchesService.findById.mockRejectedValue(
        new NotFoundException('Branch not found.'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.organize(
          owner as any,
          productId,
          {
            branchId: anotherBranchId,
          } as any,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);

      expect(productsRepository.update).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject staff attempting to organize a product outside their branch', async () => {
      productsRepository.findById.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(
        service.organize(
          staff as any,
          productId,
          {
            stock: 20,
          } as any,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);

      expect(productsRepository.update).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should map database constraint errors during update', async () => {
      productsRepository.findById.mockResolvedValue(
        product as any,
      );

      productsRepository.update.mockRejectedValue(
        createQueryFailedError('23503'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.organize(
          owner as any,
          productId,
          {
            stock: 20,
          } as any,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(ConflictException);
    });
  });

  describe('delete', () => {
    it('should delete an existing product', async () => {
      productsRepository.findById.mockResolvedValue(
        product as any,
      );

      productsRepository.delete.mockResolvedValue(undefined);

      await expect(
        service.delete(organizationId, productId),
      ).resolves.toBeUndefined();

      expect(productsRepository.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        productId,
      );

      expect(productsRepository.delete).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        productId,
      );
    });

    it('should reject an empty organization ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.delete('', productId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);

      expect(productsRepository.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(productsRepository.delete).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject an empty product ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.delete(organizationId, ''),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);

      expect(productsRepository.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(productsRepository.delete).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should not delete a product that does not exist', async () => {
      productsRepository.findById.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(
        service.delete(organizationId, productId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new NotFoundException('Product not found.'),
      );

      expect(productsRepository.delete).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should map database constraint errors during deletion', async () => {
      productsRepository.findById.mockResolvedValue(
        product as any,
      );

      productsRepository.delete.mockRejectedValue(
        createQueryFailedError('23503'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.delete(organizationId, productId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(ConflictException);
    });
  });
});

==================================================

import {
  BadRequestException,
  ConflictException,
  ForbiddenException,
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { QueryFailedError } from 'typeorm';

import { Product } from '../../schemas/Product.schema';
import type { RequestUser } from '../../common/interfaces/request-user.interface';
import type {
  PaginationParams,
  PaginatedResult,
} from '../../common/interfaces/pagination.interface';

import { BranchesService } from '../branches/branches.service';

import { ProductsRepository } from './products.repository';
import { CreateProductDto } from './dto/create-product.dto';
import { OrganizeProductDto } from './dto/organize-product.dto';

interface PostgresError {
  code?: string;
}

@Injectable()
export class ProductsService {
  constructor(
    private readonly productsRepository: ProductsRepository,
    private readonly branchesService: BranchesService,
  ) {}

  async create(
    organizationId: string,
    dto: CreateProductDto,
  ): Promise<Product> {
    this.validateRequiredId(organizationId, 'Organization ID');
    this.validateRequiredId(dto.branchId, 'Branch ID');

    await this.branchesService.findById(
      organizationId,
      dto.branchId,
    );

    try {
      return await this.productsRepository.create(
        organizationId,
        dto.branchId,
        dto.name,
        dto.category ?? null,
        dto.stock,
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.handleDatabaseError(error);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  async findAll(
    requester: RequestUser,
    pagination: PaginationParams,
    requestedBranchId?: string,
  ): Promise<PaginatedResult<Product>> {
    this.validateRequiredId(
      requester.organizationId,
      'Organization ID',
    );

    let branchId: string | undefined;

    if (requester.userType === 'STAFF') {
      if (!requester.branchId) {
        throw new ForbiddenException(
          'Your account is not assigned to a branch.',
        );
      }

      // STAFF can only see products in their own branch.
      // Ignore any branchId supplied in the query.
      branchId = requester.branchId;
    } else if (requestedBranchId !== undefined) {
      // OWNER may filter by a branch within their organization.
      await this.branchesService.findById(
        requester.organizationId,
        requestedBranchId,
      );

      branchId = requestedBranchId;
    }

    const [items, total] =
      await this.productsRepository.findAll(
        requester.organizationId,
        pagination,
        branchId,
      );

    return {
      items,
      meta: {
        page: pagination.page,
        limit: pagination.limit,
        total,
        totalPages: Math.ceil(total / pagination.limit),
      },
    };
  }

  async findById(
    requester: RequestUser,
    productId: string,
  ): Promise<Product> {
    this.validateRequiredId(
      requester.organizationId,
      'Organization ID',
    );
    this.validateRequiredId(productId, 'Product ID');

    let branchId: string | undefined;

    if (requester.userType === 'STAFF') {
      if (!requester.branchId) {
        throw new ForbiddenException(
          'Your account is not assigned to a branch.',
        );
      }

      branchId = requester.branchId;
    }

    const product = await this.productsRepository.findById(
      requester.organizationId,
      productId,
      branchId,
    );

    if (!product) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Product not found.');
    }

    return product;
  }

  async organize(
    requester: RequestUser,
    productId: string,
    dto: OrganizeProductDto,
  ): Promise<Product> {
    this.validateRequiredId(
      requester.organizationId,
      'Organization ID',
    );
    this.validateRequiredId(productId, 'Product ID');

    if (
      dto.category === undefined &&
      dto.stock === undefined &&
      dto.branchId === undefined
    ) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        'At least one field must be provided.',
      );
    }

    // First enforce tenant and STAFF branch visibility.
    const product = await this.findById(
      requester,
      productId,
    );

    // Validate the destination branch if the product is moving.
    if (dto.branchId !== undefined) {
      await this.branchesService.findById(
        requester.organizationId,
        dto.branchId,
      );
    }

    const updates: Partial<
      Pick<Product, 'category' | 'stock' | 'branchId'>
    > = {};

    if (dto.category !== undefined) {
      updates.category = dto.category;
    }

    if (dto.stock !== undefined) {
      updates.stock = dto.stock;
    }

    if (dto.branchId !== undefined) {
      updates.branchId = dto.branchId;
    }

    try {
      return await this.productsRepository.update(
        product,
        updates,
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.handleDatabaseError(error);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  async delete(
    organizationId: string,
    productId: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    this.validateRequiredId(organizationId, 'Organization ID');
    this.validateRequiredId(productId, 'Product ID');

    const product = await this.productsRepository.findById(
      organizationId,
      productId,
    );

    if (!product) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Product not found.');
    }

    try {
      await this.productsRepository.delete(
        organizationId,
        productId,
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.handleDatabaseError(error);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  private validateRequiredId(
    value: string | null | undefined,
    fieldName: string,
  ): void {
    if (typeof value !== 'string' || !value.trim()) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        `${fieldName} is required.`,
      );
    }
  }

  private handleDatabaseError(error: unknown): void {
    if (!(error instanceof QueryFailedError)) {
      return;
    }

    const databaseError =
      error.driverError as PostgresError | undefined;

    switch (databaseError?.code) {
      case '23503':
        throw new ConflictException(
          'This product is currently in use and cannot be modified or deleted.',
        );

      case '23505':
        throw new ConflictException(
          'A database constraint was violated.',
        );

      case '22P02':
        throw new BadRequestException(
          'Invalid identifier.',
        );

      case '22003':
        throw new BadRequestException(
          'Numeric value is out of range.',
        );
    }
  }
}

import { ApiProperty, ApiPropertyOptional } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { Transform, Type } from 'class-transformer';
import {
  IsInt,
  IsOptional,
  IsString,
  IsUUID,
  Length,
  Max,
  Min,
} from 'class-validator';

import { NoLeadingSpecialChars } from '../../../common/decorators/noLeadingSpecialChars';

export class CreateProductDto {
  @ApiProperty({
    example: 'Wireless Mouse',
    maxLength: 100,
  })
  @Transform(({ value }) =>
    typeof value === 'string' ? value.trim() : value,
  )
  @IsString()
  @Length(1, 100)
  @NoLeadingSpecialChars()
  name!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ format: 'uuid' })
  @IsUUID()
  branchId!: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    example: 'Electronics',
    maxLength: 50,
    nullable: true,
  })
  @IsOptional()
  @Transform(({ value }) =>
    typeof value === 'string' ? value.trim() : value,
  )
  @IsString()
  @Length(1, 50)
  category?: string | null;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    example: 100,
    default: 0,
    minimum: 0,
    maximum: 2147483647,
  })
  @IsOptional()
  @Type(() => Number)
  @IsInt()
  @Min(0)
  @Max(2147483647)
  stock = 0;
}

import { ApiPropertyOptional } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { Transform, Type } from 'class-transformer';
import {
  IsInt,
  IsUUID,
  IsString,
  Length,
  Max,
  Min,
  ValidateIf,
} from 'class-validator';

export class OrganizeProductDto {
  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    example: 'Accessories',
    maxLength: 50,
  })
  @ValidateIf((_object, value) => value !== undefined)
  @Transform(({ value }) =>
    typeof value === 'string' ? value.trim() : value,
  )
  @IsString()
  @Length(1, 50)
  category?: string;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({
    example: 75,
    minimum: 0,
    maximum: 2147483647,
  })
  @ValidateIf((_object, value) => value !== undefined)
  @Type(() => Number)
  @IsInt()
  @Min(0)
  @Max(2147483647)
  stock?: number;

  @ApiPropertyOptional({ format: 'uuid' })
  @ValidateIf((_object, value) => value !== undefined)
  @IsUUID()
  branchId?: string;
}

import {
  Body,
  Controller,
  Delete,
  Get,
  HttpCode,
  HttpStatus,
  Param,
  ParseUUIDPipe,
  Patch,
  Post,
  Query,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import {
  ApiBearerAuth,
  ApiOperation,
  ApiTags,
} from '@nestjs/swagger';

import { GetUser } from '../../common/request-metadata/getUser.decorator';
import type { RequestUser } from '../../common/interfaces/request-user.interface';
import type { PaginatedResult } from '../../common/interfaces/pagination.interface';
import { PERMISSIONS } from '../../common/constants/permissions.constants';

import {
  OwnerOnly,
} from '../auth/decorators/auth.decorator';
import {
  RequirePermissions,
} from '../auth/decorators/permissions.decorator';

import { UsersService } from './users.service';
import { CreateStaffDto } from './dto/create-staff.dto';
import { UpdateStaffDto } from './dto/update-staff.dto';
import { StaffQueryDto } from './dto/staff-query.dto';
import { UserResponseDto } from './dto/user-response.dto';
import { User } from '../../schemas/User.schema';

@ApiTags('Users')
@ApiBearerAuth()
@Controller('users')
export class UsersController {
  constructor(
    private readonly usersService: UsersService,
  ) {}

  @Post()
  @OwnerOnly()
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Create a staff member' })
  async createStaff(
    @GetUser() requester: RequestUser,
    @Body() dto: CreateStaffDto,
  ): Promise<UserResponseDto> {
    const user = await this.usersService.createStaff(
      requester.organizationId,
      dto,
    );

    return this.toResponse(user);
  }

  @Get()
  @RequirePermissions(PERMISSIONS.STAFF_VIEW)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get paginated staff members' })
  async findAllStaff(
    @GetUser() requester: RequestUser,
    @Query() query: StaffQueryDto,
  ): Promise<PaginatedResult<UserResponseDto>> {
    const result = await this.usersService.findAllStaff(
      requester,
      {
        page: query.page,
        limit: query.limit,
      },
    );

    return {
      items: result.items.map((user) => this.toResponse(user)),
      meta: result.meta,
    };
  }

  @Get(':id')
  @RequirePermissions(PERMISSIONS.STAFF_VIEW)
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get a staff member by ID' })
  async findStaffById(
    @GetUser() requester: RequestUser,
    @Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) userId: string,
  ): Promise<UserResponseDto> {
    const user = await this.usersService.findStaffById(
      requester,
      userId,
    );

    return this.toResponse(user);
  }

  @Patch(':id')
  @OwnerOnly()
  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Update a staff member' })
  async updateStaff(
    @GetUser() requester: RequestUser,
    @Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) userId: string,
    @Body() dto: UpdateStaffDto,
  ): Promise<UserResponseDto> {
    const user = await this.usersService.updateStaff(
      requester.organizationId,
      userId,
      dto,
    );

    return this.toResponse(user);
  }

  
@Delete(':id')
@OwnerOnly()
@HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
@ApiOperation({ summary: 'Delete a staff member' })
async deleteStaff(
  @GetUser() requester: RequestUser,
  @Param('id', ParseUUIDPipe) userId: string,
): Promise<{ message: string }> {
  await this.usersService.deleteStaff(
    requester.organizationId,
    userId,
  );

  return {
    message: 'Staff member has been deleted successfully.',
  };
}


  private toResponse(user: User): UserResponseDto {
    return {
      id: user.id,
      organizationId: user.organizationId,
      name: user.name,
      email: user.email,
      userType: user.userType,
      branchId: user.branchId,
      roleId: user.roleId,
      createdAt: user.createdAt,
      updatedAt: user.updatedAt,
    };
  }
}

  imports: [
    TypeOrmModule.forFeature([User]),
    CommonModule,
    BranchesModule,
    RbacModule,
  ],

  providers: [UsersRepository, UsersService],

import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';
import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
import {
  EntityManager,
  Repository,
} from 'typeorm';

import { User } from '../../schemas/User.schema';
import type { PaginationParams } from '../../common/interfaces/pagination.interface';

@Injectable()
export class UsersRepository {
  constructor(
    @InjectRepository(User)
    private readonly repository: Repository<User>,
  ) {}

  async createOwner(
    organizationId: string,
    name: string,
    email: string,
    passwordHash: string,
    manager?: EntityManager,
  ): Promise<User> {
    const repository = manager
      ? manager.getRepository(User)
      : this.repository;

    const user = repository.create({
      organizationId,
      name,
      email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
      passwordHash,
      userType: 'OWNER',
      branchId: null,
      roleId: null,
    });

    return repository.save(user);
  }

  async createStaff(
    organizationId: string,
    name: string,
    email: string,
    passwordHash: string,
    branchId: string,
    roleId: string,
    manager?: EntityManager,
  ): Promise<User> {
    const repository = manager
      ? manager.getRepository(User)
      : this.repository;

    const user = repository.create({
      organizationId,
      name,
      email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
      passwordHash,
      userType: 'STAFF',
      branchId,
      roleId,
    });

    return repository.save(user);
  }

  async findByEmail(email: string): Promise<User | null> {
    return this.repository
      .createQueryBuilder('user')
      .addSelect('user.passwordHash')
      .where('user.email = :email', {
        email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
      })
      .getOne();
  }

  async findAuthUserById(userId: string): Promise<User | null> {
    return this.repository.findOne({
      where: { id: userId },
    });
  }

  async findById(
    organizationId: string,
    userId: string,
  ): Promise<User | null> {
    return this.repository.findOne({
      where: {
        id: userId,
        organizationId,
      },
    });
  }

  async findAllStaff(
    organizationId: string,
    pagination: PaginationParams,
    branchId?: string,
  ): Promise<[User[], number]> {
    const query = this.repository
      .createQueryBuilder('user')
      .where('user.organizationId = :organizationId', {
        organizationId,
      })
      .andWhere('user.userType = :userType', {
        userType: 'STAFF',
      });

    if (branchId !== undefined) {
      query.andWhere('user.branchId = :branchId', {
        branchId,
      });
    }

    return query
      .orderBy('user.createdAt', 'ASC')
      .addOrderBy('user.id', 'ASC')
      .skip((pagination.page - 1) * pagination.limit)
      .take(pagination.limit)
      .getManyAndCount();
  }

  async findStaffById(
    organizationId: string,
    userId: string,
    branchId?: string,
  ): Promise<User | null> {
    const query = this.repository
      .createQueryBuilder('user')
      .where('user.id = :userId', { userId })
      .andWhere('user.organizationId = :organizationId', {
        organizationId,
      })
      .andWhere('user.userType = :userType', {
        userType: 'STAFF',
      });

    if (branchId !== undefined) {
      query.andWhere('user.branchId = :branchId', {
        branchId,
      });
    }

    return query.getOne();
  }

  async updateStaff(
    user: User,
    updates: Partial<Pick<User, 'name' | 'branchId' | 'roleId'>>,
  ): Promise<User> {
    Object.assign(user, updates);

    return this.repository.save(user);
  }

  async deleteStaff(
    organizationId: string,
    userId: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    await this.repository.delete({
      id: userId,
      organizationId,
      userType: 'STAFF',
    });
  }
}

FILE: C:\Users\maeed\OneDrive\Documents\Projects\Branchwork\src\modules\users\users.service.spec.ts
==================================================

import {
  BadRequestException,
  ConflictException,
  ForbiddenException,
  NotFoundException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { QueryFailedError } from 'typeorm';

import { CryptoService } from '../../common/services/crypto.service';
import { BranchesService } from '../branches/branches.service';
import { RbacService } from '../auth/rbac.service';
import { UsersRepository } from './users.repository';
import { UsersService } from './users.service';

function createQueryFailedError(code: string): QueryFailedError {
  const driverError = Object.assign(
    new Error('Database query failed.'),
    { code },
  );

  return new QueryFailedError(
    'TEST QUERY',
    [],
    driverError,
  );
}

describe('UsersService', () => {
  let service: UsersService;

  let usersRepository: jest.Mocked<
    Pick<
      UsersRepository,
      | 'createOwner'
      | 'createStaff'
      | 'findByEmail'
      | 'findAuthUserById'
      | 'findById'
      | 'findAllStaff'
      | 'findStaffById'
      | 'updateStaff'
      | 'deleteStaff'
    >
  >;

  let branchesService: jest.Mocked<
    Pick<BranchesService, 'findById'>
  >;

  let rbacService: jest.Mocked<
    Pick<RbacService, 'findRoleById'>
  >;

  let cryptoService: jest.Mocked<
    Pick<CryptoService, 'hashPassword'>
  >;

  const organizationId = 'organization-123';
  const branchId = 'branch-123';
  const anotherBranchId = 'branch-456';
  const roleId = 'role-123';
  const anotherRoleId = 'role-456';
  const userId = 'staff-123';

  const owner = {
    id: 'owner-123',
    organizationId,
    name: 'John Owner',
    email: 'owner@example.com',
    userType: 'OWNER',
    passwordHash: 'hashed-owner-password',
    branchId: null,
    roleId: null,
  };

  const staff = {
    id: userId,
    organizationId,
    name: 'John Staff',
    email: 'staff@example.com',
    userType: 'STAFF',
    passwordHash: 'hashed-staff-password',
    branchId,
    roleId,
  };

  const requesterOwner = {
    id: owner.id,
    organizationId,
    userType: 'OWNER',
    branchId: null,
    roleId: null,
  };

  const requesterStaff = {
    id: 'another-staff-123',
    organizationId,
    userType: 'STAFF',
    branchId,
    roleId,
  };

  const pagination = {
    page: 1,
    limit: 10,
  };

  beforeEach(() => {
    usersRepository = {
      createOwner: jest.fn(),
      createStaff: jest.fn(),
      findByEmail: jest.fn(),
      findAuthUserById: jest.fn(),
      findById: jest.fn(),
      findAllStaff: jest.fn(),
      findStaffById: jest.fn(),
      updateStaff: jest.fn(),
      deleteStaff: jest.fn(),
    };

    branchesService = {
      findById: jest.fn(),
    };

    rbacService = {
      findRoleById: jest.fn(),
    };

    cryptoService = {
      hashPassword: jest.fn(),
    };

    service = new UsersService(
      usersRepository as unknown as UsersRepository,
      branchesService as unknown as BranchesService,
      rbacService as unknown as RbacService,
      cryptoService as unknown as CryptoService,
    );
  });

  describe('createOwner', () => {
    it('should create an owner successfully', async () => {
      usersRepository.createOwner.mockResolvedValue(
        owner as any,
      );

      const result = await service.createOwner(
        organizationId,
        owner.name,
        owner.email,
        owner.passwordHash,
      );

      expect(usersRepository.createOwner).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        owner.name,
        owner.email,
        owner.passwordHash,
        undefined,
      );

      expect(result).toEqual(owner);
    });

    it('should pass the transaction manager to the repository', async () => {
      const manager = {} as any;

      usersRepository.createOwner.mockResolvedValue(
        owner as any,
      );

      await service.createOwner(
        organizationId,
        owner.name,
        owner.email,
        owner.passwordHash,
        manager,
      );

      expect(usersRepository.createOwner).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        owner.name,
        owner.email,
        owner.passwordHash,
        manager,
      );
    });

    it('should reject an empty organization ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.createOwner(
          '',
          owner.name,
          owner.email,
          owner.passwordHash,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new BadRequestException(
          'Organization ID is required.',
        ),
      );

      expect(usersRepository.createOwner).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should map duplicate email errors to ConflictException', async () => {
      usersRepository.createOwner.mockRejectedValue(
        createQueryFailedError('23505'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.createOwner(
          organizationId,
          owner.name,
          owner.email,
          owner.passwordHash,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new ConflictException(
          'A user with this email already exists.',
        ),
      );
    });

    it('should propagate unexpected errors', async () => {
      const error = new Error('Unexpected repository error.');

      usersRepository.createOwner.mockRejectedValue(error);

      await expect(
        service.createOwner(
          organizationId,
          owner.name,
          owner.email,
          owner.passwordHash,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(error);
    });
  });

  describe('createStaff', () => {
    const dto = {
      name: 'John Staff',
      email: 'staff@example.com',
      password: 'StrongPassword123!',
      branchId,
      roleId,
    };

    it('should create staff successfully', async () => {
      cryptoService.hashPassword.mockResolvedValue(
        'hashed-password',
      );

      usersRepository.createStaff.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      const result = await service.createStaff(
        organizationId,
        dto as any,
      );

      expect(branchesService.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        branchId,
      );

      expect(rbacService.findRoleById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        roleId,
      );

      expect(cryptoService.hashPassword).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        dto.password,
      );

      expect(usersRepository.createStaff).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        dto.name,
        dto.email,
        'hashed-password',
        branchId,
        roleId,
      );

      expect(result).toEqual(staff);
    });

    it('should reject an empty organization ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.createStaff('', dto as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new BadRequestException(
          'Organization ID is required.',
        ),
      );

      expect(branchesService.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(usersRepository.createStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject an empty branch ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.createStaff(organizationId, {
          ...dto,
          branchId: '',
        } as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new BadRequestException('Branch ID is required.'),
      );

      expect(branchesService.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(usersRepository.createStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject an empty role ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.createStaff(organizationId, {
          ...dto,
          roleId: '',
        } as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new BadRequestException('Role ID is required.'),
      );

      expect(rbacService.findRoleById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(usersRepository.createStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should not create staff when the branch does not belong to the organization', async () => {
      branchesService.findById.mockRejectedValue(
        new NotFoundException('Branch not found.'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.createStaff(organizationId, dto as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);

      expect(rbacService.findRoleById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(cryptoService.hashPassword).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(usersRepository.createStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should not create staff when the role does not belong to the organization', async () => {
      rbacService.findRoleById.mockRejectedValue(
        new NotFoundException('Role not found.'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.createStaff(organizationId, dto as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);

      expect(cryptoService.hashPassword).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(usersRepository.createStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should not persist staff when password hashing fails', async () => {
      const error = new Error('Password hashing failed.');

      cryptoService.hashPassword.mockRejectedValue(error);

      await expect(
        service.createStaff(organizationId, dto as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(error);

      expect(usersRepository.createStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should map duplicate email errors to ConflictException', async () => {
      cryptoService.hashPassword.mockResolvedValue(
        'hashed-password',
      );

      usersRepository.createStaff.mockRejectedValue(
        createQueryFailedError('23505'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.createStaff(organizationId, dto as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(ConflictException);
    });
  });

  describe('findByEmail', () => {
    it('should return a user matching the email', async () => {
      usersRepository.findByEmail.mockResolvedValue(
        owner as any,
      );

      const result = await service.findByEmail(owner.email);

      expect(usersRepository.findByEmail).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        owner.email,
      );

      expect(result).toEqual(owner);
    });

    it('should return null when no user matches the email', async () => {
      usersRepository.findByEmail.mockResolvedValue(null);

      const result = await service.findByEmail(
        'unknown@example.com',
      );

      expect(result).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  describe('findAuthUserById', () => {
    it('should return the authentication user', async () => {
      usersRepository.findAuthUserById.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      const result = await service.findAuthUserById(userId);

      expect(
        usersRepository.findAuthUserById,
      ).toHaveBeenCalledWith(userId);

      expect(result).toEqual(staff);
    });

    it('should return null when the user does not exist', async () => {
      usersRepository.findAuthUserById.mockResolvedValue(null);

      const result = await service.findAuthUserById(
        'unknown-user',
      );

      expect(result).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  describe('findById', () => {
    it('should return a user within the organization', async () => {
      usersRepository.findById.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      const result = await service.findById(
        organizationId,
        userId,
      );

      expect(usersRepository.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        userId,
      );

      expect(result).toEqual(staff);
    });

    it('should reject an empty organization ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.findById('', userId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);

      expect(usersRepository.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject an empty user ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.findById(organizationId, ''),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new BadRequestException('User ID is required.'),
      );

      expect(usersRepository.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException when the user does not exist', async () => {
      usersRepository.findById.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(
        service.findById(organizationId, userId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new NotFoundException('User not found.'),
      );
    });
  });

  describe('findAllStaff', () => {
    it('should return staff with pagination metadata for an owner', async () => {
      usersRepository.findAllStaff.mockResolvedValue([
        [staff as any],
        25,
      ]);

      const result = await service.findAllStaff(
        requesterOwner as any,
        pagination,
      );

      expect(usersRepository.findAllStaff).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        pagination,
        undefined,
      );

      expect(result).toEqual({
        items: [staff],
        meta: {
          page: 1,
          limit: 10,
          total: 25,
          totalPages: 3,
        },
      });
    });

    it('should restrict staff to their assigned branch', async () => {
      usersRepository.findAllStaff.mockResolvedValue([
        [staff as any],
        1,
      ]);

      await service.findAllStaff(
        requesterStaff as any,
        pagination,
      );

      expect(usersRepository.findAllStaff).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        pagination,
        branchId,
      );
    });

    it('should reject staff without an assigned branch', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.findAllStaff(
          {
            ...requesterStaff,
            branchId: null,
          } as any,
          pagination,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new ForbiddenException(
          'Your account is not assigned to a branch.',
        ),
      );

      expect(usersRepository.findAllStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject an empty organization ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.findAllStaff(
          {
            ...requesterOwner,
            organizationId: '',
          } as any,
          pagination,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);

      expect(usersRepository.findAllStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should return an empty list when no staff members exist', async () => {
      usersRepository.findAllStaff.mockResolvedValue([
        [],
        0,
      ]);

      const result = await service.findAllStaff(
        requesterOwner as any,
        pagination,
      );

      expect(result).toEqual({
        items: [],
        meta: {
          page: 1,
          limit: 10,
          total: 0,
          totalPages: 0,
        },
      });
    });
  });

  describe('findStaffById', () => {
    it('should return a staff member for an owner', async () => {
      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      const result = await service.findStaffById(
        requesterOwner as any,
        userId,
      );

      expect(usersRepository.findStaffById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        userId,
        undefined,
      );

      expect(result).toEqual(staff);
    });

    it('should restrict staff lookup to the requesters branch', async () => {
      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      await service.findStaffById(
        requesterStaff as any,
        userId,
      );

      expect(usersRepository.findStaffById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        userId,
        branchId,
      );
    });

    it('should reject staff without an assigned branch', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.findStaffById(
          {
            ...requesterStaff,
            branchId: null,
          } as any,
          userId,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(ForbiddenException);

      expect(usersRepository.findStaffById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject an empty user ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.findStaffById(
          requesterOwner as any,
          '',
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);

      expect(usersRepository.findStaffById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException when the staff member does not exist', async () => {
      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(
        service.findStaffById(
          requesterOwner as any,
          userId,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new NotFoundException('Staff member not found.'),
      );
    });
  });

  describe('updateStaff', () => {
    it('should update a staff member name', async () => {
      const updatedStaff = {
        ...staff,
        name: 'Updated Name',
      };

      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      usersRepository.updateStaff.mockResolvedValue(
        updatedStaff as any,
      );

      const result = await service.updateStaff(
        organizationId,
        userId,
        {
          name: 'Updated Name',
        } as any,
      );

      expect(branchesService.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        branchId,
      );

      expect(rbacService.findRoleById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        roleId,
      );

      expect(usersRepository.updateStaff).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        staff,
        {
          name: 'Updated Name',
        },
      );

      expect(result).toEqual(updatedStaff);
    });

    it('should update the assigned branch', async () => {
      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      usersRepository.updateStaff.mockResolvedValue({
        ...staff,
        branchId: anotherBranchId,
      } as any);

      await service.updateStaff(
        organizationId,
        userId,
        {
          branchId: anotherBranchId,
        } as any,
      );

      expect(branchesService.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        anotherBranchId,
      );

      expect(rbacService.findRoleById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        roleId,
      );

      expect(usersRepository.updateStaff).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        staff,
        {
          branchId: anotherBranchId,
        },
      );
    });

    it('should update the assigned role', async () => {
      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      usersRepository.updateStaff.mockResolvedValue({
        ...staff,
        roleId: anotherRoleId,
      } as any);

      await service.updateStaff(
        organizationId,
        userId,
        {
          roleId: anotherRoleId,
        } as any,
      );

      expect(branchesService.findById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        branchId,
      );

      expect(rbacService.findRoleById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        anotherRoleId,
      );

      expect(usersRepository.updateStaff).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        staff,
        {
          roleId: anotherRoleId,
        },
      );
    });

    it('should update multiple fields together', async () => {
      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      usersRepository.updateStaff.mockResolvedValue({
        ...staff,
        name: 'Updated Name',
        branchId: anotherBranchId,
        roleId: anotherRoleId,
      } as any);

      await service.updateStaff(
        organizationId,
        userId,
        {
          name: 'Updated Name',
          branchId: anotherBranchId,
          roleId: anotherRoleId,
        } as any,
      );

      expect(usersRepository.updateStaff).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        staff,
        {
          name: 'Updated Name',
          branchId: anotherBranchId,
          roleId: anotherRoleId,
        },
      );
    });

    it('should reject an empty organization ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.updateStaff('', userId, {
          name: 'Updated Name',
        } as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);

      expect(usersRepository.findStaffById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject an empty user ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.updateStaff(organizationId, '', {
          name: 'Updated Name',
        } as any),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);

      expect(usersRepository.findStaffById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject an empty update DTO', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.updateStaff(
          organizationId,
          userId,
          {} as any,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new BadRequestException(
          'At least one field must be provided.',
        ),
      );

      expect(usersRepository.findStaffById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(usersRepository.updateStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should throw NotFoundException when the staff member does not exist', async () => {
      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(
        service.updateStaff(
          organizationId,
          userId,
          {
            name: 'Updated Name',
          } as any,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new NotFoundException('Staff member not found.'),
      );

      expect(branchesService.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(usersRepository.updateStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject an update when the resulting branch is missing', async () => {
      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue({
        ...staff,
        branchId: null,
      } as any);

      await expect(
        service.updateStaff(
          organizationId,
          userId,
          {
            name: 'Updated Name',
          } as any,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new BadRequestException(
          'Staff members must have both a branch and a role.',
        ),
      );

      expect(branchesService.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(usersRepository.updateStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject an update when the resulting role is missing', async () => {
      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue({
        ...staff,
        roleId: null,
      } as any);

      await expect(
        service.updateStaff(
          organizationId,
          userId,
          {
            name: 'Updated Name',
          } as any,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);

      expect(branchesService.findById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(usersRepository.updateStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should not update staff when the new branch is invalid', async () => {
      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      branchesService.findById.mockRejectedValue(
        new NotFoundException('Branch not found.'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.updateStaff(
          organizationId,
          userId,
          {
            branchId: anotherBranchId,
          } as any,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);

      expect(rbacService.findRoleById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(usersRepository.updateStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should not update staff when the new role is invalid', async () => {
      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      rbacService.findRoleById.mockRejectedValue(
        new NotFoundException('Role not found.'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.updateStaff(
          organizationId,
          userId,
          {
            roleId: anotherRoleId,
          } as any,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(NotFoundException);

      expect(usersRepository.updateStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should map duplicate email database errors to ConflictException', async () => {
      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      usersRepository.updateStaff.mockRejectedValue(
        createQueryFailedError('23505'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.updateStaff(
          organizationId,
          userId,
          {
            name: 'Updated Name',
          } as any,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(ConflictException);
    });

    it('should propagate unexpected update errors', async () => {
      const error = new Error('Unexpected update error.');

      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      usersRepository.updateStaff.mockRejectedValue(error);

      await expect(
        service.updateStaff(
          organizationId,
          userId,
          {
            name: 'Updated Name',
          } as any,
        ),
      ).rejects.toThrow(error);
    });
  });

  describe('deleteStaff', () => {
    it('should delete an existing staff member', async () => {
      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      usersRepository.deleteStaff.mockResolvedValue(undefined);

      await expect(
        service.deleteStaff(organizationId, userId),
      ).resolves.toBeUndefined();

      expect(usersRepository.findStaffById).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        userId,
      );

      expect(usersRepository.deleteStaff).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        organizationId,
        userId,
      );
    });

    it('should reject an empty organization ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.deleteStaff('', userId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);

      expect(usersRepository.findStaffById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(usersRepository.deleteStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should reject an empty user ID', async () => {
      await expect(
        service.deleteStaff(organizationId, ''),
      ).rejects.toThrow(BadRequestException);

      expect(usersRepository.findStaffById).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
      expect(usersRepository.deleteStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should not delete a staff member that does not exist', async () => {
      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue(null);

      await expect(
        service.deleteStaff(organizationId, userId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new NotFoundException('Staff member not found.'),
      );

      expect(usersRepository.deleteStaff).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it('should map foreign key database errors to ConflictException', async () => {
      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      usersRepository.deleteStaff.mockRejectedValue(
        createQueryFailedError('23503'),
      );

      await expect(
        service.deleteStaff(organizationId, userId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(
        new ConflictException(
          'This record is currently in use and cannot be modified or deleted.',
        ),
      );
    });

    it('should propagate unexpected deletion errors', async () => {
      const error = new Error('Unexpected deletion error.');

      usersRepository.findStaffById.mockResolvedValue(
        staff as any,
      );

      usersRepository.deleteStaff.mockRejectedValue(error);

      await expect(
        service.deleteStaff(organizationId, userId),
      ).rejects.toThrow(error);
    });
  });
});

import {
  BadRequestException,
  ConflictException,
  ForbiddenException,
  Injectable,
  NotFoundException,
} from '@nestjs/common';
import { EntityManager, QueryFailedError } from 'typeorm';

import { CryptoService } from '../../common/services/crypto.service';
import { User } from '../../schemas/User.schema';

import type { RequestUser } from '../../common/interfaces/request-user.interface';
import type {
  PaginationParams,
  PaginatedResult,
} from '../../common/interfaces/pagination.interface';

import { BranchesService } from '../branches/branches.service';
import { RbacService } from '../auth/rbac.service';

import { UsersRepository } from './users.repository';
import { CreateStaffDto } from './dto/create-staff.dto';
import { UpdateStaffDto } from './dto/update-staff.dto';

interface PostgresError {
  code?: string;
  constraint?: string;
}

@Injectable()
export class UsersService {
  constructor(
    private readonly usersRepository: UsersRepository,
    private readonly branchesService: BranchesService,
    private readonly rbacService: RbacService,
    private readonly cryptoService: CryptoService,
  ) {}

  async createOwner(
    organizationId: string,
    name: string,
    email: string,
    passwordHash: string,
    manager?: EntityManager,
  ): Promise<User> {
    this.validateRequiredId(organizationId, 'Organization ID');

    try {
      return await this.usersRepository.createOwner(
        organizationId,
        name,
        email,
        passwordHash,
        manager,
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.handleDatabaseError(error);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  async createStaff(
    organizationId: string,
    dto: CreateStaffDto,
  ): Promise<User> {
    this.validateRequiredId(organizationId, 'Organization ID');
    this.validateRequiredId(dto.branchId, 'Branch ID');
    this.validateRequiredId(dto.roleId, 'Role ID');

    // Validate branch ownership.
    await this.branchesService.findById(
      organizationId,
      dto.branchId,
    );

    // Validate role ownership.
    await this.rbacService.findRoleById(
      organizationId,
      dto.roleId,
    );

    // Hash the password before persistence.
    const passwordHash = await this.cryptoService.hashPassword(
      dto.password,
    );

    try {
      return await this.usersRepository.createStaff(
        organizationId,
        dto.name,
        dto.email,
        passwordHash,
        dto.branchId,
        dto.roleId,
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.handleDatabaseError(error);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  async findByEmail(email: string): Promise<User | null> {
    return this.usersRepository.findByEmail(email);
  }

  async findAuthUserById(userId: string): Promise<User | null> {
    return this.usersRepository.findAuthUserById(userId);
  }

  async findById(
    organizationId: string,
    userId: string,
  ): Promise<User> {
    this.validateRequiredId(organizationId, 'Organization ID');
    this.validateRequiredId(userId, 'User ID');

    const user = await this.usersRepository.findById(
      organizationId,
      userId,
    );

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException('User not found.');
    }

    return user;
  }

  async findAllStaff(
    requester: RequestUser,
    pagination: PaginationParams,
  ): Promise<PaginatedResult<User>> {
    this.validateRequiredId(
      requester.organizationId,
      'Organization ID',
    );

    let branchId: string | undefined;

    if (requester.userType === 'STAFF') {
      if (!requester.branchId) {
        throw new ForbiddenException(
          'Your account is not assigned to a branch.',
        );
      }

      branchId = requester.branchId;
    }

    const [items, total] =
      await this.usersRepository.findAllStaff(
        requester.organizationId,
        pagination,
        branchId,
      );

    return {
      items,
      meta: {
        page: pagination.page,
        limit: pagination.limit,
        total,
        totalPages: Math.ceil(total / pagination.limit),
      },
    };
  }

  async findStaffById(
    requester: RequestUser,
    userId: string,
  ): Promise<User> {
    this.validateRequiredId(
      requester.organizationId,
      'Organization ID',
    );
    this.validateRequiredId(userId, 'User ID');

    let branchId: string | undefined;

    if (requester.userType === 'STAFF') {
      if (!requester.branchId) {
        throw new ForbiddenException(
          'Your account is not assigned to a branch.',
        );
      }

      branchId = requester.branchId;
    }

    const user = await this.usersRepository.findStaffById(
      requester.organizationId,
      userId,
      branchId,
    );

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Staff member not found.');
    }

    return user;
  }

  async updateStaff(
    organizationId: string,
    userId: string,
    dto: UpdateStaffDto,
  ): Promise<User> {
    this.validateRequiredId(organizationId, 'Organization ID');
    this.validateRequiredId(userId, 'User ID');

    if (
      dto.name === undefined &&
      dto.branchId === undefined &&
      dto.roleId === undefined
    ) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        'At least one field must be provided.',
      );
    }

    const user = await this.usersRepository.findStaffById(
      organizationId,
      userId,
    );

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Staff member not found.');
    }

    const branchId = dto.branchId ?? user.branchId;
    const roleId = dto.roleId ?? user.roleId;

    if (!branchId || !roleId) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        'Staff members must have both a branch and a role.',
      );
    }

    // Validate both relationships before updating.
    await this.branchesService.findById(
      organizationId,
      branchId,
    );

    await this.rbacService.findRoleById(
      organizationId,
      roleId,
    );

    const updates: Partial<
      Pick<User, 'name' | 'branchId' | 'roleId'>
    > = {};

    if (dto.name !== undefined) {
      updates.name = dto.name;
    }

    if (dto.branchId !== undefined) {
      updates.branchId = dto.branchId;
    }

    if (dto.roleId !== undefined) {
      updates.roleId = dto.roleId;
    }

    try {
      return await this.usersRepository.updateStaff(
        user,
        updates,
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.handleDatabaseError(error);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  async deleteStaff(
    organizationId: string,
    userId: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    this.validateRequiredId(organizationId, 'Organization ID');
    this.validateRequiredId(userId, 'User ID');

    const user = await this.usersRepository.findStaffById(
      organizationId,
      userId,
    );

    if (!user) {
      throw new NotFoundException('Staff member not found.');
    }

    try {
      await this.usersRepository.deleteStaff(
        organizationId,
        userId,
      );
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.handleDatabaseError(error);
      throw error;
    }
  }

  private validateRequiredId(
    value: string | null | undefined,
    fieldName: string,
  ): void {
    if (typeof value !== 'string' || !value.trim()) {
      throw new BadRequestException(
        `${fieldName} is required.`,
      );
    }
  }

  private handleDatabaseError(error: unknown): void {
    if (!(error instanceof QueryFailedError)) {
      return;
    }

    const databaseError =
      error.driverError as PostgresError | undefined;

    if (!databaseError?.code) {
      throw new ConflictException(
        'A database constraint was violated.',
      );
    }

    if (databaseError.code === '23505') {
      throw new ConflictException(
        'A user with this email already exists.',
      );
    }

    if (databaseError.code === '23503') {
      throw new ConflictException(
        'This record is currently in use and cannot be modified or deleted.',
      );
    }
  }
}

import { ApiProperty } from '@nestjs/swagger';
import { Transform } from 'class-transformer';
import {
  IsEmail,
  IsString,
  IsUUID,
  Length,
  MaxLength,
  MinLength,
} from 'class-validator';

import { NoLeadingSpecialChars } from '../../../common/decorators/noLeadingSpecialChars';

export class CreateStaffDto {
  @ApiProperty({ example: 'John Doe', maxLength: 100 })
  @Transform(({ value }) =>
    typeof value === 'string' ? value.trim() : value,
  )
  @IsString()
  @Length(1, 100)
  @NoLeadingSpecialChars()
  name!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: 'john@example.com' })
  @Transform(({ value }) =>
    typeof value === 'string'
      ? value.trim().toLowerCase()
      : value,
  )
  @IsEmail()
  @MaxLength(255)
  email!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ example: 'StrongPassword123' })
  @IsString()
  @MinLength(8)
  @MaxLength(72)
  password!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ format: 'uuid' })
  @IsUUID()
  branchId!: string;

  @ApiProperty({ format: 'uuid' })
  @IsUUID()
  roleId!: string;
}
